We had the realization that we need to greatly increase the accommodations here at our temple for the thousands of your devotees from all over the world who wish to take part in the Kumbha-melā. At present these facilities are quite meager, and we hope that by your mercy they will increase in the future.

Unfortunately, most of your glories are not yet known to the people here. Therefore we need to increase their awareness of your pre-eminent position in the religious and spiritual history of the world and that of this holy town. The more your glories are spread, the more Kṛṣṇa will be satisfied, because Kṛṣṇa likes to see His pure devotees’ glories spread everywhere: *ebe yaśa ghuṣuk tribhuvana*.

Allahabad, apart from being the holy place where you received *dīkṣā* from Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, also has your heritage properties. We want to acquire these places for ISKCON—properties such as your pharmacy building, your initiation place, and your house. We also would like to establish a Rūpānuga University for spreading the teachings of Śrīla Rupa Goswami as you have given them to us. All these endeavors can meet success only with your mercy. Therefore, though we feel we are worthless souls, on this most auspicious day of your appearance we pray for your blessings upon us so that we may become worthy of your service.

Hare Kṛṣṇa!

Aspiring to be dust particles at your lotus feet, we remain  
Your servants at the ISKCON temple in Allahabad, India.  
(written by Advaita Ācārya Dāsa)

## Almaty

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

To save this fallen soul you came to Kazakhstan in the form of your books in 1989. In 1991 the Almaty temple president requested me to translate your books into Kazakh. We had the personal association of Śrīmān Gopīparāṇadhana Prabhu at the offices of the North European BBT. He was a scholar and at the same time a most humble Vaiṣṇava. Translation Questions are expertly guided by His Holiness Jayādvaita Swami, Śrīmān Draviḍa Prabhu, and other Vaiṣṇavas. By the blessings of my GBCs, the BBT, and many other devotees, the Kazakh *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* was printed in 2008. I am eternally indebted to you and request your blessings to chant sixteen rounds a day, translate, and preach from your books for your pleasure!

Your granddisciples at the ISKCON temple in Almaty, Kazakhstan.  
(written by Rādheśyāma Dāsa)

## Amravati

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, our lord and savior,

Please accept our most humble prostrated obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to you! All glories to your divine appearance day!

You have most kindly instructed us fallen souls to preach the message of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu as widely as possible. We like this assignment very much, but it is very difficult for us because we are like helpless and faithless bugs floating on our backs in a dark ocean. We beg for your causeless mercy upon us so that we can overcome our terrible inability to render service to you.

According to Śukadeva *Gosvāmī*, simply by hearing the transcendental glories of the Lord one achieves unflinching faith. So we cannot hear enough of the nectar of Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s holy name and pastimes. We aspire to the association of devotees who, like the Six Gosvāmīs of Vṛndāvana, constantly discuss and chant the glories of the Lord together. We pray that you are pleased by our insignificant attempts to serve you.

Under your shelter, Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement has been spread all over the world. Very soon everyone on this planet will be blessed by the chanting of the holy name. Because the spreading of the holy names is the desire of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya, your efforts to spread the *saṅkīrtana* movement are a great success. Your Divine Grace has been empowered by the Supreme Lord to do so. And now we feel alive and happy just trying to follow your instructions to distribute the Lord’s holy name and your transcendental books, which can deliver all fallen souls from the clutches of birth and death. Every sane man or woman who reads your books will surrender to your lotus feet.

With the blessings of Your Divine Grace, here at ISKCON Amravati our youth preaching is going well. Over the past year we were able to offer ten more students to your lotus feet as youth center residents. We hope that they will become spiritual leaders in your Society and thus bring more fallen souls to the Supreme Lord’s lotus feet. Please kindly shower your choicest blessings upon them.

We are also very happy to inform you that the members of our congregation are cooperating to move your mission forward. Several of them have started regularly offering *vapu-sevā* unto your divine *mūrti* in the temple by dressing you and offering you *ārati*. O most merciful Śrīla Prabhupāda, please forgive them for any offenses they might commit unknowingly. *Śrī-guru-caraṇe rati, ei se uttama-gati.*

The congregation has also enthusiastically taken up the responsibilities of different departmental services, including running the Sunday Love Feast, the BBT stall, book distribution, and many others. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless them to serve ISKCON more efficiently.

Most importantly, we have started a preacher training forum consisting of temple and congregational devotees. We hold regular *Bhagavad-gītā* courses and Bhakti Vriksha programs at different locales. O greatest preacher of *gaura-vāṇī,* Śrīla Prabhupāda, please kindly empower us so that we can become humble instruments in your hands and those of the previous *ācāryas,* and thus convince people of your message.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us the shade of your lotus feet so that we can follow in the footsteps of Your Divine Grace and previous *ācāryas* and thus please Guru and *Gaurāṅga*. Our aspiration is to mold our lives according to the four basic principles you enunciated:

1\) Books are the Basis

2\) Utility is the Principle

3\) Purity is the Force

4\) Preaching is the Essence

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us that we may be attracted to bathe in your mercy, even if only for a short time. We beg to remain wholeheartedly your menial servants, and the servants of your servants, eternally.

Your insignificant children,  
The devotees at ISKCON’s temple in Amravati, India.  
(written by Anantaśeṣa Dāsa)

## Aravade

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

The past year has seen great developments at your temple here. By your special mercy on us, we were able to achieve success in several areas.

On the temple premises we have constructed a big multipurpose hall to enhance our preaching activities. Above the hall we are constructing a guesthouse to accommodate many visiting devotees and life members. In Sangli, a nearby town where we have been preaching for ten years, we held a big Ratha-yātrā festival. Even though this was our first Ratha-yātrā there, by your grace it went extremely smoothly.

In March, after seeing the enthusiasm of the devotees in Sangli, we arranged a seven-day Bhāgavata-kathā *mahotsava* there. More than five thousand people came in contact with ISKCON and attended our *Bhagavad-gītā* course. We have also started a new weekly program for them.

This is the twelfth year of our small *padayātrā,* which we take every year from Aravade to Pandharpur, where Lord Viṭṭhala resides.

Every month we invite a senior preacher to meet with members of our congregation from twelve Nāmahaṭṭa centers and teach them preaching skills.

This year we plan to expand our preaching activities in various small villages near Aravade. There was a severe drought here last year, but the devotees continued co-operating by following in your footsteps. You showed us by your example that in any situation we have to depend on Kṛṣṇa and serve Him for His pleasure.

We fall at your lotus feet and pray that you will bestow your causeless mercy upon us so that we all can continue following in your footsteps.

*Your servants in* the ISKCON temple in Aravade, India.

## Athens

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

The *Pañca-tantra* tells the story of a thirsty herd of elephants that trampled a rabbit warren near a lake. The harassed rabbits sent their representative to the elephant leader. The clever rabbit pointed out to the head elephant that the moon has a rabbit in it and also appears in the lake every night. According to the rabbit, all this definitively proved that the moon was the leader of the rabbits. Since the elephants had angered the moon by trampling the rabbits, the elephants should leave the lake and never return. The elephants were convinced and left for good.

The moral of the story is that someone with a powerful leader has nothing to fear.

Like the rabbits by the lake, we are small and insignificant. But you are our powerful leader, and so we remain faithful to you and keep going in our efforts to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We have to face so many adversities in our preaching efforts. In this nondevotional environment, sometimes we have to struggle even to maintain our principal devotional practices. But somehow we manage to survive and persevere, not because we are strong or special but because your unconditional mercy somehow makes everything happen. We perceive your presence in your books and in our efforts to follow your instructions, to love, respect, and cooperate with your representatives and our godbrothers and -sisters in ISKCON, and to remain friendly and compassionate toward all living entities.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our powerful leader. Please lead us back home, back to Godhead. We are following behind you, trying to take with us as many people as we can.

May the moon of your merciful personality shine in the hearts of all.

Your servants at the ISKCON temple in Athens, Greece.  
(written by Nāma-saṅkīrtana Dāsa)

## Austin

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**A Plea for Your Association**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving the order to make ISKCON’s Austin center a grand success. This order is your kindness upon us, giving us a way to be connected with you constantly—with all of our thoughts, words, and deeds. This center began in a very dynamic way in the spring of 1971, when His Holiness Viṣṇujana Swami came here alone. He had no material facilities—no money, no vehicle, no helpers, not even a pair of *karatālas*. Yet simply by standing on the sidewalk and singing the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* in ecstasy while clapping his hands, he quickly attracted a following. And within a few weeks he was able to rent a temple house at 2906 Dancy Street, near the University of Texas campus, and soon the temple was filled up with ecstatic new devotees. This was all due to your amazing potency, Śrīla Prabhupāda, manifested through your dear servant.

The ISKCON center in Austin was the first one to be visited by a mayor, when Mayor Roy Butler attended the first Vedic fire sacrifice ceremony ever held in the city. On that occasion Pulastya Dāsa, Parāśara Dāsa, and Saṅkarṣaṇa Dāsa were initiated as your disciples. In the presence of Viṣṇujana Mahārāja, who was constantly meditating on and glorifying Your Divine Grace, the devotees recevied expert guidance in the science of *bhakti*.

Since Viṣṇujana Mahārāja left Austin more than forty years ago to open more temples on your graceful instructions, ISKCON Austin has opened and closed many times under varying circumstances. But since 1992 Saṅkarṣaṇa Dāsa Prabhu and I, along with a few sincere devotees Your Divine Grace has sent to assist us, have maintained regular preaching programs here. Today ISKCON Austin holds regular Sunday feasts, *harināma*-*saṅkīrtana*, and book distribution and celebrates auspicious days like Janmāṣṭamī, Vyāsa-pūjā, and Gaura Pūrṇimā.

In 1972 you wrote to Saṅkarṣaṇa Prabhu and told him that if he liked you would come to Austin. Although as things worked out Your Divine Grace wasn’t able to come then, we would very much like for you to come now and permanently remain with us, blessing us with your divine presence. There is nothing more wonderful, Śrīla Prabhupāda, than your divine association. Therefore we are begging you to shower us with it at every moment.

You taught us about the importance of divine association on 20 Februrary 1969, during a *Bhagavad-gītā* lecture in Los Angeles:

If you want divine life, if you want spiritual elevation back to Godhead, back to home—that means eternal life, blissful life full of knowledge—then you have to train yourselves in divine consciousness, or Kṛṣṇa consciousness. That you can very easily do by association. *Saṅgāt sañjāyate kāmaḥ*. If you keep divine association, then your consciousness is made divine.

So please come and bless us with your divine association. Śrīla Prabhupāda, your mercy is all that we are made of.

Your followers at the ISKCON temple in Austin, Texas, USA.  
*(*written by Viṣṇupriyā Devī Dāsī)

## Baltimore

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet! All glories to your most soothing smile! All glories to your mesmerizing, enchanting eyes! All glories to your thunderbolt lectures! All glories to your ISKCON! All glories to your Ratha-yātrā festivals! All glories to your transcendental books! All glories to your disciples! All glories to Your Divine Grace!

We were sleeping on the lap of *Māyā,* hoping against hope to enjoy this material energy. It was by your causeless mercy and the mercy of your disciples and granddisciples that we opened our eyes and saw our real position of suffering. The more we follow your instructions, the more your glories are visible. It is not possible for our small brain to even conceptualize how you achieved all that you did in this world.

We pray to you that you somehow engage us in your *saṅkīrtana* movement so that we may not deviate from this path of purification that keeps us aloof from the influence of *Māyā* and Kali-yuga.

In *The Nectar of Devotion* you write, “*The Nectar of Devotion* will teach us how to turn the one switch that will immediately brighten everything, everywhere. One who does not know this method is missing the point of life.” You have demonstrated by your own example how to turn on that one switch of loving Kṛṣṇa to brighten everything. Having missed this point of life for millions of lifetimes, we find that our life has suddenly become fortunate due to your divine appearance on this planet. All glories to you and your ISKCON movement!

Surrender to Kṛṣṇa is what you taught and demonstrated throughout your life. You want us to hear Kṛṣṇa’s name with attention. You are eternally present in your *vāṇī*. Thus you are still with us, and you will always remain our foremost *śikṣā*-*guru,* our pure guide, and our shelter. We can always approach you and receive divine inspiration and encouragement from your teachings. We pray that the devotees realize the treasure in your books, and that you give me the intelligence to increase the devotees’ enthusiasm for taking shelter of your words.

Your temples are spiritual embassies, and you are the ambassador. If anyone wants to go to the spiritual world, you are the one who can grant the visa. Also, one has to buy the currency of the spiritual world by converting mundane currency into spiritual currency by rendering devotional service, chanting sixteen rounds daily, and following the rules and regulations of the embassy. Kṛṣṇa belongs to you. You have Kṛṣṇa, and only you can give Kṛṣṇa to us. You are uniquely empowered, a *śaktyāveṣa-avatāra*. You have been empowered by Kṛṣṇa to manifest *bhakti* in the hearts of the whole world: *kṛṣṇa-śakti vinā nahe tāra pravartana*. The only prerequisite for becoming a recipient of that *bhakti* is the willingness to accept and act upon your descending mercy. That’s equivalent to attaining the greed that is the only price for real *bhakti:* *tatra laulyam api mūlyam ekalam*.

The fruit of your mercy-creeper has manifested as multitudes of happy devotees worldwide, and the numbers continue to increase. Thank you for giving us the association of many wonderful devotees, and for engaging us in serving in them.

We are still children in spiritual life and are trying to glorify Your Divine Grace. Without your mercy and compassion upon us, we could not even imagine the great fortune we have received. After such a long journey of millions of births in this material world, committing sinful activities and suffering, bewildered by *Māyā* all the time, we feel a great relief in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, which is the matchless gift from Your Divine Grace.

Your compassion upon us conditioned living entities is hard to imagine. You dedicated your whole life to giving the transcendental knowledge of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in order to uplift us from these sufferings and place us in our natural position at Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet. When we read the *Prabhupāda-līlāmṛta* we realized what it means that a Vaiṣṇava is *para-duḥkha-duḥkhī*. Seeing the population the world over suffering so much, you went through many tribulations to emancipate them.

Your determination and intense love for Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa are revealed in your *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* purports, which are pure transcendental nectar. Your books and lectures enable us to transcend the three modes of nature and bring us closer to the lotus feet of Govinda.

On this holy day of your appearance, we pray that you bless us with the purity and intelligence to perpetuate your grand movement according to our capacity and serve you as your worthy descendants.

Your humble servants at the ISKCON temple in Baltimore, Maryland, USA.

## Bangkok

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace! And all glories to the *śrī-kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtana* movement, which you kindly took to everyone in the whole world.

We are all trying our best to spread the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the local Thai people. Thailand is a Buddhist country, and so the local people do not easily come to Kṛṣṇa. They have no faith in a personal God. They are accustomed to silent meditation and are not fond of a vegetarian diet. So there are many obstacles to their becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious, but still we know it is possible, by your mercy.

At this time our devotees are all of Nepali and Indian origin, but we know that Kṛṣṇa is in the hearts of all living entities. We pray that we can attract the local people to the shelter of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa and His devotional service. We go on book distribution every Sunday and sometimes on other days. We distribute Thai books and invite the Thais to our center. Gradually they are coming and taking part in our *kīrtana* and tasting *prasādam*. We hope that they can seriously take up the chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa and get Lord Caitanya’s mercy, which you made available to everyone in the world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please continue to engage us in your service.

Your unworthy followers in Bangkok, Thailand.

## Bhaktivedanta Manor

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Bhaktivedanta Manor Forty Years On

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

I would like to report some of the achievements of the devotees at Bhaktivedanta Manor since you personally installed Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda on Janmāṣṭamī, 1973.

Actually, it is difficult to know where to begin, as so many things have happened in the last forty years.

But let me begin with education. First of all, I need to mention the Manor Preschool begun by Jagad-dhātrī Devī Dāsī more than twenty years ago and still going strong today. Here devotee children age two and a half to five get their first taste of Kṛṣṇa conscious learning.

Then there is the Bhaktivedanta Manor Primary School, which provides a wonderful opportunity for our children age five to eleven to experience Kṛṣṇa-centered teaching, something very dear to your heart.

Many children who go through the Manor preschool and primary school go on to become full-time devotees and responsible Kṛṣṇa conscious adults who want their children to experience the same Kṛṣṇa-centered education.

Since the 80s, when Indriyeśa Dāsa began his program of hosting public school children on visits to the Manor, more than a quarter of a million students age six and up have passed through the doors of Bhaktivedanta Manor, visiting your *darśana* room and the temple room, going for an ox ride, and taking lunch *prasādam*. And it’s not just the students who have loved every moment of their time spent here; their teachers have written letters to us effusively praising the Manor and telling how they became spiritually enlivened by the Kṛṣṇa conscious experience.

Let me not fail to mention the long, drawn-out Save-the-Manor Campaign, which began in 1982 and ended in 1996 with a complete victory. Not only were we able to keep Bhaktivedanta Manor open for public worship, but we expanded Bhaktivedanta Manor’s land from seventeen to seventy-seven acres and were able to construct a new approach road to the temple complex that met with the approval of all concerned parties. In those days the local council members were generally not very favorable to any requests from Bhaktivedanta Manor, but by the mercy of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda and you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, they have just recently approved our plan to build a community hall and associated buildings, expanding our facilities for the visiting public by two thousand square meters—a genuine miracle!

Now I must mention the cows. I remember you telling me in 1973 that real human society requires *go-rakṣa,* cow protection. You said that we ought to have 150 cows. I replied that it would be very difficult to properly care for that number of cows on our limited land. You asked how much land was required, and I replied that we would need at least one acre per cow. You responded that we should therefore purchase 150 acres. “What is the difficulty?” you said.

Today we are looking after 68 cows at the New Gokula *gośālā,* a wonderful complex of buildings where all the cows are looked after in an exemplary fashion, from the moment they’re born up to the moment they pass away. It has been rated Britain’s top *ahiṁsā* facility, i.e., place where cow protection is practiced.

I need to mention Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda, the bliss of New Gokula, who have been lovingly looked after by a team of dedicated *pūjārīs* for forty years. Their Lordships’ fame, beauty, and mercy increase year after year.

During the Janmāṣṭamī celebrations, more than 65,000 pilgrims and devotees line up for hours to have *darśana* of the Their Lordships and receive Their *mahā*-*prasādam*. More than a thousand volunteers help make this festival a huge success. On other festival days, such as Gaura Pūrṇimā, Rāma Navamī, Diwālī, and your Vyāsa-pūjā day, thousands of enthusiastic devotees, congregational members, and well-wishers pour into Bhaktivedanta Manor.

Now I wish to mention the full-time devotees living in the *āśrama* at Bhaktivedanta Manor. There is a first-class *brahmacārī* *āśrama* here. The senior *brahmacārīs* look after the juniors. *Harināma* and book distribution are emphasized, and the number of your books distributed constantly increases. There is also an active ladies’ *āśrama*, the most famous resident of which is Mother Kulāṅganā, whose dedication to preparing Their Lordships’ *maṅgala-ārati* sweets is legendary.

Another important area is grounds maintenance. *Śrīla Prabhupāda*, the grounds here at Bhaktivedanta Manor are being maintained to the highest standard, including your personal garden, the *Bhagavad-gītā* walk, and the pond area.

On another part of the estate, we have set aside three acres to growing organic vegetables for their Lordships’ offerings.

Which leads to the kitchen. Bhaktivedanta Manor is world famous for its *prasādam*. And it seems that no matter how many guests show up, there is always enough *prasādam* to feed everyone.

All these accomplishments are possible only due to the expert management and cooperation among all the departments. You will be pleased to know that the temple council and the patron council work tirelessly to ensure that Bhaktivedanta Manor is an exemplary temple.

Our 40th anniversary celebrations will continue throughout 2013. The official celebrations began on 24 March 2013 with an auspicious fire yajña, which was televised on ITV, a London-based channel with more than six million viewers.

All these achievements are possible only by your causeless compassion, your love, your personal instructions, and your divine grace in requesting Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda to take up permanent residence at Bhaktivedanta Manor.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Praying for your mercy and guidance so that we can continue to serve you and ISKCON for the rest of our lives, we remain

Your insignificant servants at Bhaktivedanta Manor, Watford, UK.  
(written by Dhanañjaya Dāsa)

## Brahmapur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

While departing from this mortal world, you requested all your disciples not to leave ISKCON for any reason. We have taken this instruction on our heads.

When we undergo pain and hardship to maintain your temples, this activity is nondifferent from serving your transcendental body.

We are about to finish constructing our *brahmacārī* *āśrama*. The slab-casting of the first floor and metalling of the ground floor of the main temple is complete. By your mercy this has been made possible, although we are but a very few sincere *brahmacārīs* and congregational members. On this auspicious occasion, we all pray at your lotus feet to bless us so that we can serve you by spreading the *saṅkīrtana* movement tirelessly.

Your insignificant servants at ISKCON Brahmapur, India.

## Brisbane

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your beautiful lotus feet.

All glories to Your Divine Loving Grace!

All glories to your Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā!

All glories to your transcendental ISKCON!

All glories to your servants who are sincerely devoted to your Hare Kṛṣṇa movement in Brisbane!

At this auspicious time of the year, on the glorious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā, it is most appropriate for all of us to take stock of our spiritual lives and see what Your Divine Grace has given us over the years, how your ISKCON movement has positively impacted all our lives, and how you have encouraged us through very difficult and challenging times in the past.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, as in many other places, here in Brisbane we also see and feel the escalating bad effects of Kali-yuga:

O learned one, in this iron age of Kali men almost always have but short lives. They are quarrelsome, lazy, misguided, unlucky, and, above all, always disturbed. \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.1.10\]

Wealth alone will be considered the sign of a man’s good birth, proper behavior, and fine qualities. And law and justice will be applied only on the basis of one’s power. \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 12.2.2\]

A sacred place will be taken to consist of no more than a reservoir of water located at a distance, and beauty will be thought to depend on one’s hairstyle. Filling the belly will become the goal of life, and one who is audacious will be accepted as truthful. He who can maintain a family will be regarded as an expert man, and the principles of religion will be observed only for the sake of reputation. \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 12.2.5\]

Yet, Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your causeless mercy we are taking advantage of the one good quality of this age: “My dear King, although Kali-yuga is an ocean of faults, there is still one good quality about this age: simply by chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra,* one can become free from material bondage and be promoted to the transcendental kingdom.” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 12.3.51)

Śrīla Prabhupāda, spreading the holy name worldwide is your greatness. Thank you for saving us all.

You have given us such a nice new location for the Brisbane temple, where our community has the opportunity and freedom to take shelter from these bad effects of Kali-yuga by engaging in devotional service to Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi.

More ills of Kali-yuga you have saved us from: “Harassed by famine and excessive taxes, people will resort to eating leaves, roots, flesh, wild honey, fruits, and seeds. Struck by drought, they will become completely ruined.” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 12.2.8)

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given the world a perfect formula for a peaceful life, even during times of crisis such as drought and famine. You taught us to live a simple life spiritualized by high thinking, taking sumptuous *kṛṣṇa*-*prasādam*, chanting the holy names of The Lord, associating with devotees, and rendering devotional services to the Deity. We should have no urge to seek shelter in other activities, which will implicate us in material bondage.

Future historians will recognize Your Divine Grace as the most brilliant spiritual personality of this age, but unfortunately most people are not able to see your brilliance right now. It is therefore our sacred duty to Your Divine Grace to broadcast your glories to the Brisbane community. The more we do that, the more the darkness of Kali-yuga will be dispelled.

*Śrīla Prabhupāda*, without your mercy, what use is another’s, and with your mercy, what use is another’s?

Your Divine Grace is giving us so many service opportunities in this Kali-yuga so that we can approach the lotus feet of Śrī Guru and Lord Kṛṣṇa and go back home to Godhead at the end of this life.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this glorious day we would like to offer you all the results of all the services at the new Brisbane temple.

More than ever before, today the Brisbane temple is nothing short of a very happy family serving together for your pleasure.

A milestone is that, with great success, the youth have taken the initiative to arrange major festivals by themselves for the pleasure of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi. This has been very uplifting for the entire Brisbane *yātrā*.

A very committed group of devotees performs steady *harināma*-*saṅkīrtana,* chanting the holy names of the Lord regularly each Friday in downtown Brisbane.

Attendance at the weekly Saturday Feast at the temple has grown and is still increasing, giving spiritual inspiration to our greater Brisbane community. This feast is complemented by the regular Sunday Soul Feast in the city at Govinda’s Restaurant. In addition, we have three *prasādam* distribution outlets in the city that continue to attract people from all walks of life and give them the chance to relish delicious, sacred *prasādam*.

The presiding Deities, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, are nicely taken care of, and the worship programs have attracted many sincere devotees to regularly offer their services in support of the worship. We also have a number of new initiates.

Knowledgeable senior members of the *yātrā* regularly conduct congregational activities and study groups in various suburbs of Brisbane.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this wonderful day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we beg that you kindly bless us with the intelligence, knowledge, strength, and courage to continue serving your movement here in Brisbane. We also solicit your blessings to build a new temple for Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi on Their own land, right next-door to the present temple.

With appreciation, affection, and respect for Your Divine Loving Grace, we remain

Your servants at the ISKCON temple in Brisbane, Australia.  
(written by Raṇacora Dāsa)

## Cape Town

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you on this very special occasion of your divine appearance on this planet! All glories to your worldwide ISKCON society!

All over the world, more and more people are coming in contact with you in various ways. People who have not been in touch with devotees before are meeting them distributing books in various places, or at festivals, or elsewhere. In Cape Town we are trying hard to follow in the footsteps of the other devotees, as we know this is what will please you. As such, we can report on many positive developments here in Cape Town. For the sake of brevity we will report on just three achievements on this occasion, the 117th anniversary of your appearance on this planet: (1) the anniversary of the Cape Town temple, (2) our most recent Ratha-yātrā, and (3) the association of visiting Vaiṣṇavas.

This year we are celebrating the twenty-fifth anniversary of the establishment of an ISKCON center in Cape Town. Over the past twenty-five years we’ve had many trials and tribulations, from the inception of the center through its evolution over the years. Somehow or other we have passed through them, and we are still here today, with our doors open and offering shelter to the conditioned souls and the devotees alike. Because of the center’s being open, *prasādam,* the Lord’s holy names, and your transcendental books are being distributed regularly. Of course, we can always do better. We pray that as we increasingly understand and surrender to your instructions it will become easier to overcome the hurdles that face us. Thus we hope we can still be here in another twenty-five years to report back to you that the center has grown even further! Of course, we lack your purity, devotion, and understanding, but we are confident that simply by perfectly following the perfect master we can achieve the perfect result.

Another achievement we offer to you: Last December we celebrated Ratha-yātrā again after not having hosted the festival for a few years. While it was not quite the festival we hoped for or planned, it was still an achievement just to re-establish the festival here after a few years’ absence. The festival was very favorably received by the devotees, the community, and visitors alike. For your pleasure and the pleasure of Lord Jagannātha, Lord Baladeva, and Śrīmatī Subhadrā Devī, we hope to improve on this great preaching festival in future years.

Another important achievement last year was that we had the good fortune of getting the much-needed association of some of your very advanced, sincere, and pure children, and in some instances even their children. When we listen to these sincere disciples of yours, we get more and more insight into your perfect character and nature. As the nature of a tree becomes known by tasting its fruit, so the nature and purity of the teacher or master is known by listening to and seeing the behavior of his sincere students or disciples. Our only wish is to continue living in the association of your followers, not only because you often said we should, and not only because the *śāstras* say so, but also because it is so nice, fulfilling, and sweet to be in the presence of advanced Vaiṣṇavas.

*Śrīla Prabhupāda*, we have full confidence that if we continue co-operating to keep our center open and active and alive with festivals like Ratha-yātrā, if we seek the association of advanced devotees, and if we always remember and respect you as the head of our devotee family, we will prosper and grow spiritually. Kindly bless us that we may continue to try to please you in every way.

Your aspiring servants at the ISKCON temple in Cape Town, South Africa.  
(written by Viśvambhara Dāsa)

## Chandigarh

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Respected Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respectful obeisances unto your lotus feet. All glories unto you, who are the foremost bold and uncompromising preacher of pure devotion to Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

Your glories are unlimited, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and as we look more and more deeply into the your teachings, we become more and more convinced about that. As a humble attempt to glorify you, we will try to describe just one facet of your glories, your propagation of pure devotional service.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you planted the seed of pure devotion to Śrī Kṛṣṇa all around the world. Although there have been various preachers in history, you are the most exalted, for you are the rare *ācārya* who preached unalloyed devotion to the Supreme Lord.

While you were present on the planet, many saintly persons went to the West to preach about God, but they preached something other than unalloyed devotion to Him. Some declared themselves to be God, some proclaimed everyone to be God, some declared God to be impersonal—everything but declaring Kṛṣṇa to be the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Only you declared this.

Most preachers don’t even approach God, for they are lost in the network of the Lord’s illusory energy. And even those who do approach the Lord usually become confused about the impersonal or localized aspect of the Lord. But you proved definitively: *kṛṣṇas tu bhagavān svayam*.

It is not surprising that you chose to present the essence of all the scriptures, *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. Though the *Mahābhārata* and *Rāmāyaṇa* are quite popular in India, you did not comment on them but chose to give comment on *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and *Bhagavad-gītā* because these scriptures establish Kṛṣṇa as the Supreme Personality of Godhead very exclusively and explicitly.

The structure you defined for your ISKCON society—early-morning *Gurv-aṣṭakam* prayers, *darśana* *ārati* prayers, *guru-pūjā*, *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* class, and then in the evening the *gaura-ārati* and *Bhagavad-gītā* class—single-pointedly aims at the exclusive, explicit, and abundant glorification of the Supreme Lord and His pure devotee, the spiritual master.

It is not that you manufactured something new. Everything you gave us had already been given by the previous *ācāryas,* but you presented it to modern people in a way they could understand. As you said regarding your books, “These books will be the law books for human society for next ten thousand years.”

In your teachings, Kṛṣṇa and His pure devotees are not mere personalities but are the *key* personalities, just as in cricket we say the star player is the “man of the match.” Or as in Hindi we sometimes say, “Kṛṣṇa *hi,*” not “Kṛṣṇa *bhi,*” meaning *only* Kṛṣṇa, not *also* Kṛṣṇa. You brought out the unique attributes of Kṛṣṇa and His pure devotees in your writings, speeches, and behavior.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you knew that without this exclusive, explicit, and abundant glorification of Kṛṣṇa and His pure devotees, people in general would remain dissatisfied. You elaborate on this understanding in your purports to several verses in the fifth chapter of the First Canto of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* wherein Śrīla Nārada Muni reveals to his disciple Śrīla Vyāsadeva the root cause of the latter’s despondency. Here is text 14 and part of your purport:

\[Nārada says to Vyāsadeva:\] Whatever you desire to describe that is separate in vision from the Lord simply reacts with different forms and names to agitate the mind, as the wind agitates a boat which has no resting place.

\[Purport:\] Śrī Vyāsadeva is the editor of all descriptions of the Vedic literatures, and thus he has described transcendental realization in different ways, namely by fruitive activities, speculative knowledge, mystic power, and devotional service. Besides that, in his various *Purāṇas* he has recommended the worship of so many demigods in different forms and names. The result is that people in general are puzzled how to fix their minds in the service of the Lord; they are always disturbed about finding the real path of self-realization. Śrīla Nāradadeva is stressing this particular defect in the Vedic literatures compiled by Vyāsadeva, and thus he is trying to emphasize describing everything in relation with the Supreme Lord, and no one else. Pouring water on the root is the right process to water the tree, as much as feeding the stomach supplies energy to all parts of the body. Therefore, Śrīla Vyāsadeva should not have compiled any *Purāṇas* other than the *Bhāgavata Purāṇa* because a slight deviation from that may create havoc for self-realization.

Now, some may say that one will not find many takers of this philosophy of exclusively and explicitly serving Kṛṣṇa and no one else, and so one should soften the message. “Too fanatical,” others might say. You gave your bold and unambiguous reply to such arguments in the Preface to your *Bhagavad-gītā* *As It Is:* “The Lord wants this, and He demands it.”

In presenting the superexcellent philosophy of the *Bhagavad-gītā,* you desired that everyone in the world take advantage of it, be purified, and become pure devotees. But showing your virtue of detachment, you said you would be satisfied if even one soul took to the process with all sincerity: “We hope, therefore, that people will derive the greatest benefit by studying *Bhagavad-gītā* *As It Is* as we have presented it here, and if even one man becomes a pure devotee of the Lord, we shall consider our attempt a success.” (Preface to *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is*)

To ensure that we had full access to this heritage of pure devotion, you gave us everything we needed: your personal example, your teachings recorded in books and other media, your ISKCON institution, and most importantly your beloved followers. Through the association of devotees who are living examples of these teachings, by your grace we have complete access to these teachings.

Although you laid out the teachings of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in your books, people in general need to see the teachings being practiced to feel inspired to follow them. As Kṛṣṇa states in the *Bhagavad-gītā* (3.21):

*yad yad ācarati śreṣṭhas tat tad evetaro janaḥ*  
*sa yat pramāṇaṁ kurute lokas tad anuvartate*

**“Whatever action a great man performs, common men follow. And whatever standards he sets by exemplary acts, all the world pursues.” From your purport: “People in general always require a leader who can teach the public by practical behavior.”**

You have given us the association of your wonderful followers, who demonstrate to us your teachings by practical behavior. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us access to the *paramparā* and keeping the *paramparā* system alive. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us access to unalloyed, unmotivated, and uninterrupted devotional service to the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. On this day of your holy appearance, we seek your blessings to be able to relish your message of exclusive, explicit, and abundant glorification of the Supreme Lord and His wonderful servitors.

You may be pleased to know, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that your center here in Chandigarh is doing fine. We are a small center but are moving forward steadily. We added a Nāma-haṭṭa center last year and distributed 36,000 copies of *Bhagavad-gītā* *As It Is,* 8,000 more than the previous year. We also conducted five Ratha-yātrā festivals last year, two more than in previous years. In 2012 108 new devotees surrendered themselves to your mission after taking *harināma* *dīkṣā*. We hosted two seminars by senior Vaiṣṇavas, one on devotional behavior (*bhakti sadācāra*) and the other on home schooling. More than seventy devotees took advantage of these seminars. As per your instructions, we are taking good care of training the *brahmacārīs* and congregational devotees in the areas of *siddhānta,* *sādhana,* *sadācāra,* and *sevā*. To meet the spiritual needs of children of all ages, we run a Gopal Fun School, a Bhakta Prahlād School, and ISKCON Youth Forum programs every week. Many children and their parents are taking a keen interest in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

We have been able to do all this only by your blessings, the guidance of senior Vaiṣṇavas, and the mercy of our presiding Deities, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava. On this most auspicious day we beg for your mercy so that we may become eternal instruments in the service of your mission.

Your servants at ISKCON Chandigarh, India.

## Chennai

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear worshipable Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All glories to you! All glories to Śrī Guru and Śrī *Gaurāṅga*!

Feeling blessed by you, we offer you this appreciation of just a few of your wonderful attributes.

Your preaching is wonderful.  
Your mission is wonderful.  
Your disciples are wonderful.  
Your methods of convincing others are wonderful.  
Your intentions are wonderful.  
Your selfless services to the world are wonderful.  
Your books are wonderful.  
Your teachings are wonderful.  
Your words are wonderful.  
Your “thunderbolts” are wonderful.  
Your *kīrtanas* are wonderful.  
Your *bhajans* are wonderful.  
Your lectures are wonderful.  
Your morning walks are wonderful.  
Your letters to your disciples are wonderful.  
Your initiations of your disciples are wonderful.  
Your counseling is wonderful.  
Your heart is wonderful.  
Your mercy is wonderful.  
Your chanting is wonderful.  
Your taking care of your disciples is wonderful.  
Your “simply wonderfuls” are wonderful.  
Your cooking is wonderful.  
Your distribution of *prasādam* is wonderful.  
Your humility is wonderful.  
Your tolerance is wonderful.  
Your mastership is wonderful.  
Your vision is wonderful.  
Your smile is wonderful.  
Your guidance is wonderful.  
Your compassion is wonderful.  
Your behavior is wonderful.

In short, everything about you and everything and everyone connected to you is wonderful, and anyone disconnected from you is extremely unfortunate.

On this supremely auspicious day of your appearance, we pray at your lotus feet that we may never become disconnected from you, that we may never miss even a day of *sādhana,* and that we may never displease you at any time during the rest of our lives.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
All glories to Śrī Guru and Śrī Gaurāṅga!  
All glories to all the Vaiṣṇavas!

Your devotees at the ISKCON temple in Chennai, India.  
(written by Parameśvara Dāsa)

## Chicago

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**I was born in the darkest ignorance, but my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torch of knowledge. I offer my respectful obeisances unto him.**

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī** ***Gosvāmī*****. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanya and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. Please accept us as eternal servants of your servants.

You came into our lives like an unexpected rainstorm. We were a flock of hollow *cakora* birds crying to an empty sky, our feet scorched by the desert of the material world. We were suffering the reactions due us, having been born in this iron age of Kali—short lives, endless argument, lethargy, misdirection, misfortune, and harassment on all sides by the senses and the mind. We were degraded souls seeking refuge in delusory shelters*.* In your *Nectar of Instruction* (7, purport) you sum up our plight:

In the material world everyone is trying to become happy, and although their material attempts are baffled in every way, due to their nescience they cannot understand their mistakes. People try to rectify one mistake by making another mistake. This is the way of the struggle for existence in the material world.

Our lives were exactly as you describe. Trapped in the desert of this material world, we were chasing drops of happiness in the form of family life, friends, and society. None of us had heard the song of Lord Kṛṣṇa. Who knew how futile were our attempts to make a permanent, happy home in this anityam asukhaṁ lokam? Or that our attempt to live happily ever after in martyaloka was like trying to build a castle in the sky? Only you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We did not know any better. And that is why you came to save us.

Many of us sought shelter from the scorching heat of material life through prayer to a God we did not fully understand. We had never heard of the āśraya-vigraha, the Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead, the manifestation of the Lord of whom one must take shelter. Suddenly you appeared in our lives as an emissary descended directly from the spiritual world, carrying with you the ocean that is the Lord’s mercy. By your divine instructions you connected us to the Lord’s pleasure-giving potency, and it was like tasting water for the very first time. To this day we are still quenching our thirst in the ocean of your transcendental guidance, and we are inviting others to come and take shelter in the shade of your lotus feet. Your lessons untied the complex knots in our hearts that bound us to our gross bodies and filthy minds. You taught us the simple principle that by practicing humility, submission, and loving service we can avoid the unnecessary struggle for existence in the wasteland of this material world. In one sentence you encapsulate the essence of our faults: “When one gives up his unwanted attitude of superiority, he becomes situated in his original position.” (Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam 4.28.64, purport). Through your personal example you showed us how surrender and obedience to Kṛṣṇa and the spiritual master destroy all unwanted things in the heart of the sincere sādhaka and bestow the bliss of spiritual life.

At a time when we were stumbling through darkness, blindly following the dictates of modern scientists, absurdist philosophers, and our own juvenile minds, you arrived like the rising sun and taught us that enlighten and everything we could ever long for exist within one prayer—Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, bless us that we may shed our offensive nature and see Kṛṣṇa in the holy name in this lifetime, or in some future birth. We pray that, on the strength of your mercy, we may one day abandon the subtle sex desire of hankering after recognition, distinction, and worship, that we may give up malice, envy, miserliness in distributing the holy names, and all other forms of violence to other living entities, that we may transcend the intoxication of ahaṅkāra and the foolish notion that we can accomplish anything without the mercy of Kṛṣṇa, the founder-ācārya, our spiritual masters, and the community of Vaiṣṇavas, and finally that we may firmly resolve to stop gambling with this human form of life by maintaining affection for mundane activities and the treacherous opinions of worldly-minded persons. In this way, may we deeply take to heart the regulative principles of freedom, which you laid before us and which bestow upon the earnest fractioned the complete mercy of the Lord, as Kṛṣṇa promises Arjuna: vidheyātmā prasādam adhigacchati. (Bhagavad-gītā 2.64).

Somehow or other the ISKCON Chicago community is now experiencing an unprecedented torrential downpour of your mercy. Together, you and Kṛṣṇa have blessed us with unlimited opportunities to expand our services. Within the past year devotees have taken inspiration from other ISKCON communities in North America and started several exciting projects meant to share Kṛṣṇa consciousness in wider circles and more deeply transform the lives of those we contact. In the spring of 2012 we started hosting a Monthly Saṅkīrtana Festival and Nāma-yajña to increase our commitment to distributing your books and performing harināma saṅkīrtana for our own purification and for the spiritual upliftment of the entire Chicago community. Last autumn we also inaugurated Kṛṣṇa Lunch, an ambitious university prasādam distribution project. It has now spread to three universities in Chicago, and we are distributing more than four hundred boxed lunches per week. In the fall we also opened the Vaiṣṇava Academy to provide Kṛṣṇa conscious education for the youth in our community so that they would not have to endure the nightmare of the modern educational system. And just this spring we held our first weekly Bhakti Śāstrī classes for congregational members, with nearly a hundred devotees signed up to participate. For the upcoming fall semester at the Illinois Institute of Technology, we will be officially opening Chicago’s first Kṛṣṇa House, a place where spiritually inclined students can come to take shelter from the hell of exams, term papers, and wild intoxication. Finally, we are seeing more and more young men and women show an interest in real spiritual life, and as a result our brahmacārī āśrama and greater temple community are steadily growing, both quantitatively and qualitatively, as the need to practically demonstrate the lifestyle of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to others increases. We can only conclude that the frequent presence of your beloved spiritual sons and daughters—Romapāda Swami; Candramauli Swami; Rādhānātha Swami; Dānavīr Swami; Bīr Kṛṣṇa Swami; Mother Dānakelī and Pṛthu Śrava Prabhu; Vaiśeṣika Prabhu; Baḍa Hari Prabhu; Draviḍa Prabhu; and so many others—has sufficiently purified our community so as to attract the attention of Your Divine Grace, as well as Kṛṣṇa Himself, and that by the Lord’s glance our devotional service is flourishing. Otherwise, in every respect we are most unqualified, and our collective attempt to push on your Hare Kṛṣṇa movement leaves much to be desired.Have mercy on us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, so that we may continue to work together to expand your International Society for Krishna Consciousness and ourselves take full shelter of the oases you established, like miniature Vaikuṇṭha-lokas, in the desert of this material world.

*hā hā! prabhu prabhupāda rākha pāda-dvandve*  
*kṛpā-dṛṣṭe cāha jadi haiyā ānande*

*mano-vāñchā siddhi tabe haṅa pūrṇa-tṛṣṇa*  
*hethāya caitanya mile sethā rādhā-kṛṣṇa*

**O Śrīla Prabhupāda, O master, please place us at your lotus feet. If you are pleased with us, please cast your merciful glance upon us. Only if you are satisfied with us will all our heartfelt desires be fulfilled and our thirst for happiness satiated. Only if you are satisfied with us will we attain the association of Lord Caitanya here and that of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa in Goloka. (Prārthanā, modified).**

Your servants at the ISKCON temple in Chicago, Illinois, USA.

## Columbus

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, who has opened my eyes, which were blinded by the darkness of ignorance, with the torchlight of knowledge.**

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of** ***Bhaktisiddhānta*** **Sarasvatī** ***Gosvāmī*****. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances.

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

I am very elated to have the opportunity to write our simple offering to you, *Śrīla Prabhupāda*, the glorious *ācārya* who are the emblem of oceanic compassion. In the mood of Śrī *Nityānanda* Prabhu, you spread the message of *patita-pāvana* Śrī Gauracandra throughout the world. Who can possibly understand the mood of our beloved Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Naṭabara and their pure devotees unless they are blessed and empowered by them? Many wonderful devotees—gurus, *sannyāsīs,* GBCs, temple presidents, and other leaders—have written offerings to you for this occasion, and I feel like a crow in the assembly of swans as I attempt to write an offering on behalf of our temple. In all honesty, I can only pray sincerely for the proper mood, intelligence, and consciousness to express some of my feelings of gratitude as an offering to you, our illustrious Founder-*ācārya*, Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad Abhaya Caraṇarāvinda Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda. Please forgive my shortcomings in writing this offering. Totally unqualified, I have gotten this opportunity only by the causeless mercy of the Vaiṣṇavas.

When you visited Ohio State University in May 1969, with your ecstatic *kīrtana* you inspired more than two thousand students at Hitchcock Hall to chant and dance. At that time you said we should have an ISKCON center in Columbus. A pure devotee’s words never go in vain. Soon, desiring to please you, your disciples and well-wishers started a center near the OSU campus to distribute the mercy of Śrī Gaura-Nitāi by the simple yet powerful means of chanting the holy name, feasting on *kṛṣṇa*-*prasādam*, and dancing. The *saṅkīrtana* movement then took off in Columbus.

During the decades that followed, many devotees have taken the responsibility of overseeing the Columbus temple, doing their best to serve you and your mission by reaching out to as many people as possible with the gift of *bhakti*. They left their legacy to us, the current temple management comprised of your grandchildren, to do our best to continue this *saṅkīrtana* movement here. We are humbly trying to follow in the footsteps of our predecessors, to please them and please you. We have a long way to go, but on this auspicious occasion we beg you for the strength, direction, inspiration, determination, and eagerness to spread this movement as a pleasing service to you.

Please use us as your instruments to fearlessly spread the glories of the holy name and convince people of the importance of *bhakti-yoga*. Chanting the holy name offenselessly, studying and distributing your books, associating with devotees, nicely worshiping the Deities, serving the devotees—only by these and similar services will we please you as well as Śrī *Śrī* Guru and *Gaurāṅga,* thus enabling us to get out of this material world and go back home, back to Godhead. It has been proven time and time again that your vision, your strategy, and the principles you taught through your books and other media are priceless and universal. Indeed, they can be applied anytime and anywhere. We don’t have to add or subtract anything. As long as we stick to your principles, we will be successful; otherwise, as we have witnessed, there will be deviations, misunderstandings, disunity, etc. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please save us by guiding us in such a way that we will never leave the path of *bhakti* as you have taught it. One of your last instructions to us was “You will show your love for me by how you cooperate with each other after I’m gone.” It is very difficult to always cooperate with other devotees, but such cooperation is the very sustenance of our spiritual life. Cooperation is an austerity only because we are always prone to egoistic and petty, self-centered dealings. Please forgive us. We hope that one day, by your mercy, we will be able to overcome selfishness and please you by fully cooperating in pushing on this movement, as you wanted.

Despite all our personal and collective challenges, we are having good success in the Columbus center. This success is possible only by your causeless mercy and the reciprocation from their all-merciful Lordships Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā, and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Naṭabara. The temple is managed by a council comprised of Prema Sindhu Dāsa, Prema Vilāsa Dāsa, Rāma Tīrtha Dāsa, and myself, Naveen Kṛṣṇa Dāsa, under the able guidance of your beloved daughter Her Grace Mālatī Devī Dāsī. Responsibilities are further divided into various departments headed by inspired devotees who continue your legacy and try to maintain the standards you gave us. To highlight some of them: The book distribution and motel *Gītā* program headed by Prema Vilāsa and Prema Sindhu Prabhus are exceptional. Despite having full-time jobs and families, they distribute books door to door and set up book tables in different places on various occasions to reach out to sincere seekers of the Absolute Truth. They are supported by many inspired congregational members. Preaching programs at the temple include the Sunday Feast, headed by Rāma Tīrtha Prabhu, and the Thursday Program, headed by Harināmānanda Prabhu. We also conduct Bhakti Vriksha programs and OSU campus cooking classes, overseen by Dāmodara Priya Prabhu and me. In addition, when other organizations request our presence on various occasions, Dāmodara and I represent ISKCON. The daily *japa* conferences and *Gītā* classes conducted by Prema Sindhu Prabhu enliven many devotees to grow spiritually strong.

Another beloved daughter of yours, Her Grace Kāmagiri Devī Dāsī, tirelessly yet lovingly serves the Deities and devotees by cooking every Thursday and Sunday, and for all festivals. She has been doing this service for many years despite declining health.

Our Deity department is headed up by Rāma Tīrtha Prabhu, with the assistance of Tapasvinī Devī Dāsī, Lalitā Devī Dāsī, and other congregational members, including Yādavācārya Prabhu, Sunītā Devī Dāsī, Prema Sindhu Prabhu, Kālindī Sundarī Devī Dāsī, Jagannātha Prabhu, *Sītā* Sundarī Devī Dāsī, Vrajabhakti Devī Dāsī, Candra Bhānu Prabhu, Līlā Mādhurī Devī Dāsī, Kṛśāṅgī Sakhī Devī Dāsī, Arjuna Sakha Prabhu, Suri Prabhu, Senthil Prabhu, and many other devotees, who serve the Deities by dressing them weekly or during festivals. It is glorious to see these wonderful devotees serving your mission by following your divine instructions to the best of their capacity. Please shower your mercy on them and their family members so that they can continue serving for the rest of their lives.

Other devotees, such as Govinda Prabhu, Harināmānanda Prabhu, Deet Prabhu, and Hari Prabhu, lead heart-melting *kīrtanas* and *bhajans* to inspire the devotees. We also have other departments and services taken care of by devotees who always stay behind to run the temple, do purchasing or cleaning, or handle the finances. The Sunday school is headed up by Rāsa Vilāsinī Devī Dāsī and Vrajabhakti Devī Dāsī, with the help of other devotees.

Recently Prema Vilāsa prabhu and Lalitā Devī Dāsī made a tremendous sacrifice by donating a substantial sum of money to renovate the temple basement, which now looks like Vaikuṇṭha. Now the Deities and devotees have comfortable facilities, such as a new walk-in cooler to store *bhoga* and flowers, two full bathrooms, a storage room, a children’s room, new heating and cooling facilities, a well-organized Deity room with cabinets and shelves to store the Deity paraphernalia very nicely, and a renovated kitchen to make it more effective and efficient. They also took care of the backyard and garden, turning it into a Vṛndāvana garden with their love for you. Please shower your mercy on them.

Rāma Tīrtha Prabhu, with able guidance from Prema Vilāsa Prabhu, led the finance department to a very stable position and also paid off the mortgage, which is a huge milestone for us. New devotees such as Sanjiv, Mamata, Sanjay, Pinky, Rajesh, Silan, Richa, Pavitra, Kirti, Senthil, Prema, Parth, Sreeram, Jyothsna, and Jayendran are doing wonderful services for your mission. It is very amazing to see how you inspire these devotees. On a daily and weekly basis the flower garlands are offered to you, and all the Deities’ necessities are purchased and prepared, by ever-dependable devotees such as Tapasvinī, Harināmānanda, Parth, *Sītā*, Sundarī, Vrajabhakti, Kruti, and Bhavani. Also, we have a few devotees who graduated from Columbus and moved on to their next destination to continue their devotional life, such as Pitambar, Gokul Vilasini, Madan Sundar, and Sundar Prabhu.

The list can go on, but none of this would be possible without your mercy and the seed of devotion you have planted in all of us, either directly or indirectly. Caitanya Mahāprabhu said He was a gardener distributing the love-of-Godhead fruit freely to whoever wanted it. You, as His personal associate, are in the mood of the previous *ācāryas,* fulfilling His desire selflessly. We only hope that one day we can live up to your expectations by developing wonderful and sweet relationships among us, by setting an example for future generations of devotees, so they will appreciate and take inspiration from you and your mission and accept responsibility for carrying on your mission in the future. We also have a plan to move to a larger facility to serve your mission better by accommodating more people and making happier devotees, but we need your blessings to make it happen. If we have the blessing of the Vaiṣṇavas, everything is possible.

On this auspicious day commemorating your appearance, please accept our offering of gratitude. Thank you very much for everything you have done and are doing for us.

Your devotees at the ISKCON Krishna House in Columbus, Ohio.  
(written by Naveen Kṛṣṇa Dāsa)

## Coventry

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

Years go by, but tirelessly you keep giving your mercy to all those who are dedicated to your movement. This most beautiful religion of love is holistic, progressive, and all-inclusive. Only those who read your books and preach realize your endless mercy despite facing all the challenges of life. The world may fault others, but no one should find even a minute fault in your selfless character and actions, which are ideal and exemplary.

You are the best thing that has happened to our lives. By your grace we enjoy *kṛṣṇa-kathā*, Deity worship, chanting, *kīrtanas*, visits to the holy *dhāmas,* and association with other *Vaiṣṇavas*. ISKCON temples are opening, especially in Bhārata-varṣa, and it brings joy to our hearts. More and more sincere people are taking to Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and they will further influence others in promoting Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

In Coventry we have faced many serious challenges, perhaps much as other *yātrās* have, but we have had some success in overcoming these seemingly insurmountable hurdles. Two things have given us immense confidence: your life-size form in our temple, and your divine books. Many a time the battles are not with outsiders but with those within the institution, and those difficulties are the most challenging. As some of the older members have walked away, many new people have arrived, desiring to learn about the real purpose of life. This is both a great surprise and a great joy for us.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, when we read that all the great saints had to face incredible challenges and criticisms, which you also faced, we gain faith that everything good or bad is arranged by the all-merciful Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

One of the greatest gifts you gave the world is Lord *Caitanya*. If you had not come to the West, we in the West would have simply wasted our lives. Hearing Lord *Caitanya*’s pastimes makes your movement a historical spiritual revolution. Lord *Nityānanda*’s mercy is limitless and available to all. The inhabitants of this world will have a chance to go back home, back to Godhead, only because you disseminated the mercy of Lord *Nityānanda* and Lord *Caitanya*. As we hear more and more accounts of Lord *Caitanya*’s and Lord Nityānanda’s glorious lives, we are inspired to chant the *mahā-mantra* in a loving manner and also to engage others in chanting it.

As time passes we are increasingly impressed and amazed by your mercy, even though we never met you. Our never having met you does not mean we receive less of your mercy. Your methods of organizing ISKCON, your spiritual guidance, and your wisdom are so up-to-date and relevant for modern times that all succeeding generations will be inspired by you for thousands of years. We are proud to know you and your special movement. You are a true *ācārya,* Śrīla Prabhupāda, and we in Coventry love you dearly:

An *ācārya* is one who fully understands the conclusions of the revealed scriptures and whose behavior reflects his deep realization. He is a living example, for he teaches the meaning of the scriptures by both word and deed. \[*Vāyu Purāṇa*\]

Your humble servants and aspiring servants at ISKCON Coventry, UK.  
(written by Hari Dāsa)

## Czech Republic

An offering to His Divine Grace Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, Founder-*Ācārya* of ISKCON, from the servants and the servants of the servants of His Divine Grace and Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Navadvīpacandra in the Czech Republic, on the occasion of the celebration of the 117th anniversary of His Divine Grace’s appearance day.

\* \* \*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We became devotees of Lord Kṛṣṇa  
by your divine grace,  
and we continue to be devotees of Lord Kṛṣṇa  
only by your divine grace.  
The divine grace  
with which Lord Kṛṣṇa, the divine Supreme Personality of Godhead,  
has empowered you  
is fathomless, transcendental,  
inconceivable for materialists.  
It cannot be bought, it cannot be sold,  
it cannot be imitated,  
it has no material cause whatsoever,  
it is causeless.  
Before you came from India to America in 1965,  
together with Lord Kṛṣṇa  
in the form of His holy name and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*,  
you wrote to a politician in India  
that you were going back to Godhead in this life,  
and that you were very eager to take as many people as possible  
back to Godhead with you.  
That is the essence of your divine grace,  
your mercy, your compassion.  
Why were you convinced that you were returning back to Godhead?  
Because Lord Kṛṣṇa told you personally,  
by His divine grace.  
You knew Lord Kṛṣṇa personally, intimately,  
by His divine grace.  
He spoke with you, personally, because you are His dearmost friend;  
you spoke with Him, personally, because you are His most confidential servant.  
In fact, you are the most powerful person in the world  
because you are a confidential servant of the all-powerful person,  
the Lord of the universe, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Kṛṣṇa.  
We bow our heads at your lotus feet—humbly, sincerely—  
Because we are insignificant fallen souls.  
We have no power or capacity to go back to Godhead.  
We therefore pray that you and your confidential servants  
kindly take us back to Godhead,  
by your divine grace.

\* \* \*

All glories to His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s servants in the Czech Republic.  
(written by Jaygurudeva Dāsa)

## Detroit

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Every year on this most auspicious day, all around the world, your beloved disciples, granddisciples, and followers eagerly glorify you and, for your pleasure, offer heartfelt reports of their activities in devotional service. Your divine family is growing every day because your empowered leaders and their dedicated teams of devotees are constantly working to save more and more souls from the clutches of Māyā. Your Divine Grace established more than 108 temples all around the world. Among them, Devasadan Mandir here in Detroit is among your most beloved projects, one where you showered special mercy. You called Śrī Kuñjabihārījī Bhakta-vatsala because He leans His head toward His dearmost devotee, Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī, so much so that it seems as though He might touch Her. You created a Vaikuṇṭha atmosphere here by your special pastimes. You planted the seeds of bhakti by meeting many prominent personalities and attracting them toward Kṛṣṇa.

Here at your Devasadan Mandir we are celebrating the fortieth anniversary of the installation of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kuñjabihārī throughout the year, with many special events planned to please you.

With the blessings of our beloved GBC, Her Grace Mālatī Devī Dāsī, we started the celebration on Govardhana Pūjā day with ecstatic kīrtana. All the devotees prayed together to increase the distribution of your books and participation in harināmas and Bhakti-vriksha and other outreach programs. This year, for the pleasure of the Deities and the devotees, we are striving to double the participation at every festival, and we have added two big festivals: a Puṣpābhiśeka festival and a Gopāṣṭamī festival.

The number of guests at our Ratha-yātrā has doubled since we moved it to the city of Novi.

Two new Bhakti-vriksha groups have been added to expand our outreach.

Renovation teams have been continually upgrading our facilities to please the Deities and better accommodate devotees and guests.

A new electronic welcome center has been established to introduce every guest to the temple programs.

A new state-of-the-art donation box has been acquired.

Thus even throughout the economic downturn of the last five years, which has been especially severe in Detroit, your Devasadan Mandir has grown steadily. Depending on the mercy of Kṛṣṇa, a principle you taught us by word and deed, we are finding more and more opportunities to expand our preaching efforts.

O ever well-wisher of every living entity, please continue showering your mercy on us so that we can continue serving you and our most merciful Deities, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kuñjabihārījī, until we leave these bodies. Please hold our hand tight, because we have a tendency to run away from you.

Begging for forgiveness for all our offenses, we remain

Your humble servants at Devasadan Mandir, Detroit, Michigan, USA.  
(written by Jagad-Guru Dāsa)

## Durban

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We bow down to you. Please accept our gratitude for your mercy, our only sustenance.

Often we read or hear of how you would eagerly listen to preaching reports from all over the world. They would give you life. They would make you smile. Please accept this offering as an attempt to evoke that same all-merciful smile.

Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rādhānātha remain gorgeously dressed and sumptuously fed. This exquisite temple is still a source of wonder for the many tourists and devotees who visit. Nāma-haṭṭas push forward. College preaching programs expand and improve, linking a variety of people to Kṛṣṇa. Thousands of plates of *prasādam* are distributed weekly to students and others. *Harināma* and book distribution are gaining in regularity. Over the last year the *āśrama* has expanded; more young devotees are joining, and this is inspiring.

But, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we just can’t wait to share with you our report on our 25th annual Festival of the Chariots. Your servant His Holiness Indradyumna Swami took up the phenomenal task of revolutionizing the festival so that this year we could open our arms wide and receive guests of different backgrounds and races. After two decades we returned to the most prestigious venue, the long stretch of the Durban beachfront where hundreds of thousands of tourists and locals stroll by. Many government dignitaries, such as the mayor of Durban, the premier of South Africa, and the Zulu king, have been wondering how South Africa would become a nation that was socially cohesive and how the enmity between races would cease. His Holiness Indradyumna Swami convinced them that we have the answer, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and that unity would prevail only when we saw each other as children of the same father, God.

On this premise an incredible festival, the likes of which we have never seen before, took shape. Lord Jagannātha glided down the promenade in royal style with Zulu-warrior dancers, Zulu maidens, drum majorettes, Bhārata-nāṭyam and Kathakālī dancers, bagpipers, trumpeters, and dancing Chinese dragons accompanying the *kīrtana*. Each night the main stage was packed with people eager to be part of the festival’s highlights. His Holiness Bhakti Bhṛṅga Govinda Mahārāja worked closely with the KwaMashu Gospel Choir and the KZN Philharmonic Orchestra to produce a masterful symphony accompanied by the singing of the *mahā-mantra*. It brought new attendees in the audience to tears. Government dignitaries attended and supported the vision of the festival. They danced to the beat of the holy name in the Kirtanuity tent. Prominent religious leaders also attended, building relationships with devotees, which could open many doors in the future. To appeal to the younger generation, a popular music band was invited. Holi has become famous with the youth worldwide; therefore we hosted a color festival that made the front page of a daily newspaper. It was all part of the effort to attract people who ordinarily would not come to a Hare Kṛṣṇa event and give them an opportunity to chant the holy names, make friends with the devotees, and take *prasādam*—in style.

Africa, the sleeping giant, is finally shaking off its slumber and rising to the call of Lord Caitanya. The holy name, your gift to us all, is reaching people and places we never dreamed of before. It is taking root. And we are grateful to you for allowing us to be part of this chapter of Lord Caitanya’s *līlā*. It is our privilege.

We remember how after your morning walks in Los Angeles in the early days you would exit your car and walk between two rows of kneeling devotees before entering the temple to greet the Deities.
