You knew that without the strong association of other devotees practicing Kṛṣṇa consciousness, it would be impossible for any of us to become Kṛṣṇa conscious. You knew that if you did not establish a spiritual society (ISKCON) to encourage and facilitate the association of devotees practicing devotional service, none of us could have become Kṛṣṇa conscious, for our restless minds, material attachments, and ignorance would have prevented us. As your followers, we often find it burdensome to maintain even a single temple, with its many challenges and personal conflicts. Yet out of your humility and dedication to the order of your spiritual master, you accepted the difficulties of managing a worldwide spiritual movement that provided us with many ways to engage our bodies, minds, and words in devotional service in the association of devotees.

For us to become completely Kṛṣṇa conscious, we must become fully humble, understanding that we are insignificant servants of our spiritual master. Although you showed us by your perfect example how to remain completely humble in the Lord’s service, we disciples and granddisciples of yours have a tendency to forget our tiny position, become puffed-up with false prestige, and forget Lord Kṛṣṇa. You said that the purpose of this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is to make one independently thoughtful. This is significant, because each of us, individually, must ultimately decide if we want to surrender to Lord Kṛṣṇa or not. We must become introspective, consciously analyzing how the Kṛṣṇa conscious philosophy is perfectly applicable to our personal lives. We need to always remember that Lord Kṛṣṇa is the supreme controller and enjoyer and our well-wishing friend and that He personally arranges every experience we have on this path to help us advance.

You said, “Your love for me will be tested by how well you cooperate in spreading this movement.” The way you established ISKCON, *we must work together* to carry out your instructions and spread this movement. Serving cooperatively gives us spiritual strength. If we fail in some endeavor in serving your mission, we all share that responsibility so that the burden is not so heavy that we become discouraged and leave. If we are successful in our spiritual endeavors, the glory is enhanced because we did it in cooperation with one another. And most important, if we always remember that we are dependent on one another to please you and serve your mission, we will never become bewildered by false prestige and try to take full credit for whatever little success we may have.

For example, one devotee may dress the temple Deities very beautifully and the other devotees may express their appreciation for the service. That is all right. But if the *pūjārī* who dresses the Deities starts becoming falsely proud of his service, that pride will hamper his Kṛṣṇa consciousness. To remain humble, the *pūjārī* should consider that his success in dressing the Deities is in fact the result of cooperation among many devotees because he could not have done his service without other devotees having served in the past and present. Did that morning’s *pūjārī* also sew the outfit the Deities wore? Did he make the jewelry with which he decorated the Lord? Did he make that day’s beautiful flower garlands? Did he single-handedly do everything necessary to keep the temple open over the years until the day he dressed the Deities? Without a temple, how could he have dressed the Deities? Such a simple analysis shows that no devotee is an island and that any success we may have requires the cooperation of many other devotees.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you gave us everything we need to become Kṛṣṇa conscious, even in this lifetime. Please help us follow your example of humility and work together cooperatively to spread this movement.

Your humble servants at ISKCON Miami, Florida, USA.

## Montreal

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

What to say if not an endless thank you, again and again?

You have made my life useful. Without your mercy I would be lost in this world, with no good quality whatsoever. Actually, I have often thought that without your mercy I wouldn’t have been able to live in this crazy world. Nothing material can make me happy or peaceful. Everything disturbs me, giving no satisfaction whatsoever.

Śrīla Prabhupāda you have given me everything. I feel that I am getting closer and closer to you. The only thing that matters for me now is to please you. Since I don’t know how to please Kṛṣṇa, the safest thing to do is to please you. I came to know you from your books, from your recorded lectures, morning walks, and interviews, from your letters, and from all your disciples who took the time to write their memories of the time they spent with you.

It has been over thirty-five years now since you left us, but I feel your presence in my heart more and more. Thus I do my service in the mood that you are always watching me. This gives me so much hope and satisfaction—feeling that you are living with me, that you are still here. I am learning so much in this way. The struggle against *māyā* is easier, knowing you are with me. I am getting spiritually stronger every day because of you. I am hoping to go back to Godhead, and I owe all of this to you. Śrīla Prabhupāda, I will never be able to repay you.

The secret is that every devotee can do the same.

May I always feel your presence in my heart until I die.

Śrīla Prabhupāda kī jaya!

Your servants at ISKCON Montreal, Canada.  
(written by Anubhāva Dāsa)

## Mumbai (Chowpatty)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

As years pass and we delve deeper and deeper into the meanings of your various lectures, articles, and teachings, we have no option but to be more and more humble, seeing the great magnitude of true devotional service you offered to Lord Kṛṣṇa through the *guru paramparā*. No words can express our gratitude.

Lord Kṛṣṇa states in the *Bhagavad-gītā* that the purest of all devotees, who know Vāsudeva, are never under the control of the material nature but are totally surrendered to Lord Kṛṣṇa and are under the guidance of His spiritual nature. Such *mahātmās* use one hundred percent of their energy and time only in the activities of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, carrying out the orders of their spiritual masters. You elucidate these points in your detailed purports.

Out of millions of living entities, hardly one even comes to the fundamental level of understanding Kṛṣṇa and surrendering to Him, and out of million of such devotees, only one can surrender 100% to Lord Kṛṣṇa so that te Lord fulfills all his desires.

We have seen over and over again that Lord Kṛṣṇa fulfilled all your desires, *Śrīla Prabhupāda*. To make sure your desires were fulfilled, Kṛṣṇa extended your life twice after you had nearly died (once on your sea voyage on the *Jaladuta,* once after your stroke in May 1967 in New York). Kṛṣṇa gave you just enough time to fulfill your Guru Mahārāja’s desire that you spread Lord *Caitanya’s* teachings to every town and village of the world.

I have been to every continent in the world, and I have been surprised and extremely delighted to find your books, devotees, or some of your messages even in the darkest jungles of Africa, the deserts of Asia and Australia, the remotest islands in the Pacific Ocean, and the snow-clad mountaintops of South America, Canada, etc. There are many instances where devotees have taken a cue from your teachings and example and bravely preached in extremely adverse or dangerous conditions. For example, in the 1990s devotees daily distributed *prasādam* in Bosnia during the war there. Or, even during serious floods, earthquakes, or epidemics devotees have continued to offer their best devotional services by distributing your books. And during the annual December book distribution marathon, they often rest only two or three hours a night. Such devotional feats surely confirm that you and those who carry out your orders are not under the control of Mahāmāyā but under the supervision of Yogamāyā.

Alas, when will the day come when at last ten percent of the world’s people are Kṛṣṇa conscious? Then those *śreṣṭhas* can lead the world into the ten-thousand-year golden age when everyone lives under the banner of Lord *Caitanya* and Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Praying to always stay in the service of the *guru paramparā* and Lord Kṛṣṇa, we remain

Your servants at the ISKCON temple in Chowpatty, Mumbai. India.  
(written by Śrī Nāthjī Dāsa)

## Mumbai (Juhu)

Our Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

By your divine mercy we have completed construction on the new building of the Bhaktivedanta Swami Mission School in Sashtri Nagar. We have shifted all functions of the school from Hare Krishna Land to Sashtri Nagar, and the entire academic session of 2012–13 was conducted at the new premises.

We had the official inauguration of the school on 12 January 2013, with *vastu-śānti-pūjā*. The program also included felicitation of all the donors who contributed for the construction of the new building. We also felicitated many other well-wishers who helped the project in various capacities.

On 13 January 2013, Lord Jagannātha, Lord Baladeva, and Subhadrā Devī were installed in a colorful ceremony. We ecstatically performed a *mahā-abhiṣeka* with auspicious ingredients and a *puṣpa-abhiṣeka* with fragrant flowers from our farm. Their Lordships are now being nicely worshiped by a group of dedicated devotees.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you greatly emphasized cow protection. The *gośālā* in your Talasari farm has been adopting cows abandoned on the highway or injured on the way to the slaughterhouse. A dedicated veterinarian is taking care of them. May this please Your Divine Grace. Please guide us to develop the cow protection program, which pleases Lord Govinda.

In distributing your transcendental books, ISKCON Juhu stood in the fourth place in the world for the year 2012. Our book score this year is the highest ever: 232,100 *mahā*-big books, 939 big books, 21,359 medium books, and 106,126 small books, for a grand total of 510,804 book points. We seek your blessings to increase our book distribution for your pleasure.

Year after year, the Śrī Rāma Navamī festival expands, with increasing participation of Mumbai’s Śrī Rāma *bhaktas*. This year we sent out an appeal to all patron life members and other donors asking them to contribute for the Śrī Rāma Navamī Anna Dāna Sevā. We are planning a five-day festival of Śrī Rāma Kathā and profuse *prasādam* distribution. We hope this is pleasing to you and beg your mercy for the success of the festival.

Now that the school has shifted to the new building in Sashtri Nagar, we are planning to renovate the old building. We hope to have more *āśrama* facilities for *sannyāsīs,* senior *brahmacārīs,* visiting devotees, and elderly *mātājīs*. We will also plan to have dormitory facilities for the congregation. In addition, we plan to have some offices and a fully equipped modern kitchen. We seek your guidance for proper planning and execution of this renovation work.

Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us an opportunity to serve your dear Lords, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsabihārījī.

Your insignificant servants at the ISKCON temple in Juhu, Mumbai, India.

## Mumbai (Mira Road)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

On this auspicious occasion of your divine appearance, we are grateful for the opportunity to speak a few words in praise of your infinite glories and mercy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your kindness knows no limits because you have given us so many spiritual gems. You have mercifully bestowed upon us jewels of such value that it is hard to calculate their worth. First and foremost, you have given us the holy name of Kṛṣṇa in the form of the *mahā-mantra*. What the sages strove to achieve in lifetimes you have made easily available to us by giving us the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra.

Although the great liberated souls have taken complete shelter in the holy name, which is glorified by the *Upaniṣads,* we still find excuses not to take shelter of the name. The holy name is so attractive that all the great sages chant it continuously. Chanting the holy name is so powerful that it can relieve all our sufferings.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you describe how just a dim glimmer of the holy name can deliver us from material existence. In the dark of night we may fear many things, like tigers, but as soon is there is a little hint of the sun’s light we are no longer afraid. In the same way, if even a dim reflection of the pure chanting of the holy name arises in our hearts, we no longer have to fear *māyā*. We never read that meditation on impersonal Brahman can free one from the effects of our past sins. Yet the *śāstra* declares the power of the holy name can rid the chanter of all sins accumulated for lifetimes.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this blessed day of your appearance we seek your mercy so that our attraction for the many names of Kṛṣṇa may increase. Names of Kṛṣṇa that are in relation to His beloved devotees are so dear to Him and His pure devotees. Names like Nanda-nandana and Yaśodā-nandana make the hearts of pure devotees dance with joy. Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is you who help us chant with ecstasy due to attraction for the holy names.

The worship of the Deity in the temple is a wonderful gift of yours to us, a gift of unimaginable splendor. Still, you have advised us to always chant the holy name while worshiping the Deity.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, this holy name you have given us brings us untold bliss. When we chant with the devotees, a desire to dance arises in our hearts and we forget all our material hankerings. By this chanting you have opened the doors of spiritual existence so that we can enter our real home.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your chanting is as attractive as the music from Nārada’s *vīṇā*. Your chanting has captured our hearts and continues to lead thousands of good-hearted souls into the unique loving network of Śrī *Caitanya* Mahāprabhu’s *saṅkīrtana* movement.

That brings us to the next gift you have given us: the service of Śrī *Caitanya* Mahāprabhu, the son of Śacī Devī . What an amazing life we have had, all because you have so kindly given us service in the *saṅkīrtana* movement! *Caitanya* Mahāprabhu summarized the *saṅkīrtana* movement when he explained to Prakāśānanda Sarasvatī what His spiritual master had told him:

*nāca, gāo, bhakta-saṅge kara saṅkīrtana*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāma upadeśi’ tāra’ sarva-jana*

**\[Īśvara Purī said to Lord Caitanya:\] “My dear child, continue dancing, chanting, and performing** ***saṅkīrtana*** **in association with devotees. Furthermore, go out and preach the value of chanting** ***kṛṣṇa-nāma,*** **for by this process You will be able to deliver all fallen souls.”**

From this verse we can understand the essence of Lord *Caitanya*’s *saṅkīrtana* movement. Your desire is that we dance and chant in the association of devotees and try to spread the message of the Lord to deliver all the fallen souls.

Your faith in the words of your spiritual master caused amazing things to happen. Of course, you didn’t want to take any credit for them. You built the Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma temple because you wanted to give everyone a chance to render devotional service to Kṛṣṇa in Vṛndāvana. You never thought for a moment about glory for yourself. Your motive was to please your spiritual master by spreading the *saṅkīrtana* mission of Lord *Caitanya*.

You are one of the great examples of devotees who are not satisfied simply becoming perfect themselves but rather want to see others take to the chanting of the holy name of the Lord and perfect their lives. You perfectly embody the mood of Prahlāda Mahārāja when he said:

*naivodvije para duratyaya-vaitaraṇyās*  
*tvad-vīrya-gāyana-mahāmṛta-magna-cittaḥ*  
*śoce tato vimukha-cetasa indriyārtha-*  
*māyā-sukhāya bharam udvahato vimūḍhān*

**“My dear Lord, I have no problems and want no benediction from You because I am quite satisfied to chant Your holy name. This is sufficient for me because whenever I chant I immediately merge in an ocean of transcendental bliss. I only lament to see others bereft of Your love. They are rotting in material activities for transient material pleasure and spoiling their lives toiling all day and night simply for sense gratification, with no attachment for love of Godhead. I am simply lamenting for them and devising various plans to deliver them from the clutches of māyā.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **7.9.43, as translated at Cc** ***Ādi*** **7.92, purport)**

This verse by Prahlada Mahārāja perfectly captures your mood, Śrīla Prabhupāda. How patiently you are waiting for us to catch up that same spirit! You explained to us that Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* used to say, “Even at the expense of all the properties, temples, and *maṭhas* that I have, if I could convert even one person into a pure devotee, my mission would be fulfilled.”

If we get caught up in opening temples and collecting money to maintain them and forget what you really want, then we have missed the point. The spiritual master becomes happy when he sees his disciple following the regulative principles of devotional service, chanting the prescribed number of rounds, and making advancement in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you always exhibited your complete detachment from material enjoyment and at the same time accepted everything in connection with the service of Lord Kṛṣṇa. Thus you perfectly embodied the principle of *yukta*-*vairāgya:*

*anāsaktasya viṣayān yathārham upayuñjataḥ*  
*nirbandhaḥ kṛṣṇa-sambandhe yuktaṁ vairāgyam ucyate*

**“Things should be accepted for the Lord’s service and not for one’s personal sense gratification. If one accepts something without attachment and accepts it because it is related to Kṛṣṇa, one’s renunciation is called** ***yukta-vairāgya*****.”**

*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 5.25.12 states:

*mūrdhany arpitam aṇuvat sahasra-mūrdhno*  
*bhū-golaṁ sagiri-sarit-samudra-sattvam*  
*ānantyād animita-vikramasya bhūmnaḥ*  
*ko vīryāṇy adhi gaṇayet sahasra-jihvaḥ*

**“Because the Lord is unlimited, no one can estimate His power. This entire universe, filled with its many great mountains, rivers, oceans, trees, and living entities, is resting just like an atom on one of His many thousands of hoods. Is there anyone, even with thousands of tongues, who can describe His glories?”**

Similarly, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we do not have the power to describe even a small fraction of your glories.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your vision of how to re-direct the present misguided society is very profound and far-reaching. When Lord Caitanya predicted that in the near future the day would come when the holy names of Hari would be chanted in every town and village, few believed this could ever happen. Most thought it was impossible. Although your godbrothers had such doubts, you had a clear and undeterred conviction that, by the grace of guru and Kṛṣṇa, this was indeed possible. As a soul totally surrendered to the words and desires of your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Prabhupāda, you never doubted that the holy name could be spread worldwide, and at an advanced age you ventured halfway across the globe to undertake a “mission impossible”to make true the words of both your Guru Mahārāja and Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Giridhārīs mercy we are expecting to complete the construction of a new temple for their Lordships by Kārttika this year.

What follows is the yearly preaching report of the Mumbai (Mira Road) branch of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness:

(1) Preaching to youth: Once a month we conduct a festival for college-age boys called Upasana. More than a hundred boys from various colleges attend.

(2) Preaching to children: By your mercy the devotees in our *yātrā* are planning to conduct value education classes for children in four schools. *Bhagavad-gītā* classes are conducted in two schools. Apart from these efforts, we will be having vacation batches that focus on the *Bhagavad-gītā* for children, conducted at more than twenty places, including Mira Road, Borivali, Kandivali, Jogesvari, Bhayender, Vasai, Vapi, Thane, and Kalyan. Also, we conducted Bhaktivedanta competition for students this year, and about six hundred children participated.

(3) Preaching to adults: *Brahmacārīs* and many *gṛhastha* devotees of our *yātrā* travel from Mira Road to Borivali, Kandivali, Nalasopora, Jogesvari, Bhayender, Thane, Powai, Kalyan, Vasai, Vapi, and other places to present Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

(4) Book distribution: Over the last year, many children, girls, college-age boys, *mātājīs,* and *prabhujīs* did book distribution with enthusiasm and tried to distribute in as many stations in Mumbai as possible.

(5) Jagannātha Ratha-yātrā: We held Ratha-yātrās at Mira Road, Thane, Kandivali, Borivali, Virar, Chembur, and Kalyan.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, may our words and efforts please you. But that’s “May what we want to do please you.” Rather, may our words and efforts be those you would wish. *Cittete koriyā aikya:* May our mind become one with your words, absorbed in your words, so that our efforts will always be those you would desire. May our mind be purified by taking shelter of you. Taking shelter of you is as good as taking shelter of Kṛṣṇa. And may our power of speech, instead of being engaged in words, words, words, so many useless words, always be engaged in chanting the holy names of Kṛṣṇa. May we “Chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and be happy”and make others happy by spreading the chanting of Kṛṣṇa’s holy names.

Please be merciful to us so that we may serve your instructions nicely. We beg to remain engaged in your devotional service, although we have no qualifications for such engagement. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your kind mercy upon us. Kindly forgive our shortcomings and offenses. We beg for your mercy so that we may all realize more deeply your desires to spread Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement.

We have absolutely no qualification. We are but made up of your causeless mercy. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your selfless dedication to the cause of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and the previous *ācāryas*. You are a constant source of inspiration to so many devotees all over the world, and to us as well.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, may you be pleased upon us so that we can do something to satisfy you, for only when you feel some relish from our activities can we progress in devotional service. We pray to be installed as particles of dust at your lotus feet.

Your insignificant servants at ISKCON Mira Road, Mumbai, India.

## Murmansk

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you! All glories to the *guru*-*paramparā*!

Our temple here in Murmansk—a temple we call New Koladvīpa Mandir—is the northernmost temple in Russia. By your mercy, for more than twenty years people in our harsh climate have been hearing *karatālas,* *mṛdaṅgas,* and the chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa, have had the opportunity to respect *prasādam,* and have gotten a chance to read transcendental books about the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. And for eighteen of those years our Deities—Śrī Śrī Doyāl Nitāi-Gaurasundara and Śrī Śrī Prahlāda-Nṛsiṁhadeva—have poured Their mercy on all the citizens of the Kola Peninsula. We hold programs not only in Murmansk but also in other cities and towns of the region.

Your disciples—our teachers—visit us regularly and share their transcendental experiences, teaching us how to serve Kṛṣṇa and His devotees. Last year we held the first Ratha-yātrā festival in Murmansk, and Lord Jagannātha, Lord Baladeva, and Subhadrā Devī gave their mercy to the people here. Thank you very much for the opportunity to serve devotees and Lord Kṛṣṇa. Please let us always remain in the shade of your lotus feet.

Trying to become the servants of your servants, we remain  
Your devotees in the ISKCON temple in Murmansk, Russia.  
(written by Paramānanda Dāsa)

## Nagpur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

On this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we devotees at ISKCON Nagpur would like to submit our report of last year’s activities.

We had the best December book marathon ever in the twenty-five-year history of ISKCON Nagpur, with a total of five thousand *Bhagavad-gītās* distributed. This distribution was mainly accomplished through the joint efforts of our congregational, Bhakti-vriksha teams, which at the moment number twenty. We are very confident that our results for next December’s marathon will exceed this year’s, but we know that it is only by your mercy that we will be able to please you with a new record next year.

Our systematic efforts to organize and care for the Nagpur congregation have now been underway for a little more than a year, and we are seeing the power of unity. We have a standardized curriculum for all the groups that extends over a sixty-four-week period. At the conclusion of this course the members are following the four regulative principles and chanting sixteen rounds daily. We have calculated that if we continue this program nicely for five years we will have more than five hundred groups with more than five thousand members. We want to have big *harināmas* in the streets of Nagpur and distribute books to all the residents of this important city. We want to make an undeniable impact on the collective consciousness of central India so that even the politicians here will chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and follow *sanātana-dharma*.

We know that you also want this, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, and we beg for your mercy and empowerment so that we will be able to serve you in this way. Please bless us to stay united in our attempt, because if we are united we can succeed.

We are still struggling to get land for our new temple, and we hope that by your next Vyāsa-pūjā we can report that the land has been acquired and a temple is coming up. We daily pray to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnāthajī to please help us in this endeavor to serve Them.

Finally, we are also happy to report that in the near future we will establish a first-class *brahmacārī* *āśrama,* facilitating proper training and care of the *brahmacārīs*. We are already finalizing the plans, and in a few months the first *brahmacārīs* will start their training there.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have a great hope that in the coming years we will be able to do something wonderful in Nagpur. We humbly pray to you to guide and protect us in this very difficult and challenging attempt to overcome Kali and *Māyā* and solidly establish Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the heart of India.

Please bless us, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.  
Your grandsons and -daughters at ISKCON Nagpur, India.

## New Badarikāśrama

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace and your beloved Vyāsa-pūjā day! All glories to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda!

You were so kind, tireless, and compassionate to travel the world in order to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

On June 27, 1975, you arrived in Denver for your only visit to your ISKCON center at the foothills of the Rocky Mountains. The mountains reminded you of the Himalayas, and thus you named our center New Badarikāśrama, after Śrīla Vyāsadeva’s *āśrama*.

During your arrival address you asked if anyone knew the meaning of the song by Śrīla Narottama Dāsa Ṭhākura that had just been sung to you in the temple room. As one devotee explained the meaning of the song, everyone present thought of you: “O venerable Vaiṣṇava, O ocean of mercy, please be merciful unto your servant. Please give me one drop of mercy. I pray for the shade of your lotus feet.”

We are praying like this for your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You embody the beautiful qualities of the pure Vaiṣṇava, as sung by our great Vaisnava *ācāryas*.

Then you spoke about service to the Vaisnavas, and the devotees thought of you:

So our process is very simple, that we dedicate our life to the service of Vaiṣṇava, and according to his direction, *śravaṇaṁ kīrtanam,* hearing and chanting of Viṣṇu, Kṛṣṇa, and refrain from sinful activities. Then life is successful. It is not very difficult.

Because you are the embodiment of Gaura-Nitāi’s love, in gratitude we want to dedicate ourselves purely to your mission.

The next day, on your morning walk at Denver’s City Park, you encouraged us. The devotees told you how the Sunday Feast attendance was good and how there was good book distribution in Denver. You were pleased and said:

That is the most successful. Wherever book distribution is going on nicely, that is successful, because people are in gross ignorance. They are taking this temporary life as everything. Very horrible condition. We are trying to explain what is the actual life.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are continuing to distribute your books here, but we want to increase our quantity and quality of distribution to please you.

You liked walking in Denver’s City Park. On this particular morning you told the devotees:

Chant Hare Kṛṣṇa. Very dangerous place. *Padaṁ padaṁ yad vipadam*. This is a place—in every step there is danger. We are walking in a very nice park. At any moment there may be revolution, whole thing is changed. Whole thing is—becomes fire. Just like, in India now it has become. So we should remember that here in this material world, *padaṁ padaṁ yad vipadām,* every step there is danger. Give up this place. That is the real intelligence.

Your words are always purifying to hear, enlivening to the heart, and fully imbued with compassion and love.

On your morning walk on June 30 a devotee told you about an astrologer in Māyāpur who said that you were the most powerful person in the world. You replied:

If I am representative of Kṛṣṇa, then I must be the most powerful. Kṛṣṇa has got—all omnipotent. \[laughs\] Most powerful, the most my godbrothers. That is my credit. They are thinking like that, “This man became most powerful than all of us. \[still laughing\] He was a *gṛhastha*.”

On your morning walk on July 1 you told us how we can become free of debt. Every materialist in this world wants this freedom. You gave the eternal formula and spoke in ways that no one else ever did:

Anyone who has fully surrendered to Kṛṣṇa, he is no more debtor to anyone. And Kṛṣṇa says that “You surrender to Me, and I shall keep you protected from all kinds of \[sin.\]” Because if you don’t repay your debts, then you become sinful. But Kṛṣṇa says, “I shall protect from all kinds of sin.” So if anyone has surrendered to Kṛṣṇa, he is no more debtor to anyone. He is immune from all obligation. His only obligation is to Kṛṣṇa.

We are now indebted to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please give us your eternal blessings so we may always serve your lotus feet and those of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda.

During your *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* classes you spoke from the first chapter of the Sixth Canto. On June 28 you spoke on verse 15, about *yoga-siddhis:*

So one may achieve some *yoga-siddhis;* still it is not possible to understand Kṛṣṇa. That is not possible. Kṛṣṇa can be understood only by such persons who have dedicated everything to Kṛṣṇa. Therefore, Kṛṣṇa wants that, demands, *sarva-dharmān parityajya mām ekaṁ śaraṇam.* Kṛṣṇa is only understandable by his pure devotee, not anyone else. Those who are favored by the causeless mercy of Kṛṣṇa, they can understand Kṛṣṇa.

This is our prayer, Śrīla Prabhupāda—to fully surrender to your mission.

In the same class you explained that the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is doing the topmost welfare work because we are trying to deliver Kṛṣṇa and love of Kṛṣṇa to the human society. You said that it has been effective, and today we can appreciate that the spread of Kṛṣṇa consciousness is all to your credit, though you did not take any credit for yourself. You kindly said that the European and American devotees did not previously know about Kṛṣṇa but are now serious about understanding Him. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your grace may we actually become 100% serious and dedicated.

On June 29, while speaking on *Śrīmad*-*Bhāgavatam* 6.1.16, you said that rather than working hard in the material world to become materially happy we should worship Kṛṣṇa. You stressed that we should not waste the opportunity:

Become devotee, Kṛṣṇa’s devotee. The Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is there, and we have got so many centers. Just try to learn this Kṛṣṇa consciousness science and make your life perfect. That is our request.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the words you speak glorify Kṛṣṇa, but Kṛṣṇa desires to glorify you, and so we find that every word you speak simultaneously glorifies you, because whoever constantly chants the glories of Kṛṣṇa is all-glorious.

On June 30 you spoke on *Śrīmad*-*Bhāgavatam* 6.1.17:

So *sādhu,* they have to preach. They have to preach. Therefore the first qualification is *titikṣavaḥ,* tolerant. So many people will criticize, so many people attack, so many opposing elements, and he has to fight with them. Therefore *titikṣavaḥ,* toleration.

We can understand that you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are our topmost example of such a preaching *sādhu*. May we always serve your mission.

During your class on July 1 you spoke on *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 6.1.18, instructing us with full empowerment, as Kṛṣṇa’s supreme ambassador of the holy name:

The simple process to become purified. And that simple process is this chanting. Chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, this name “Kṛṣṇa” is reminding me. I must hear and I must chant with the tongue. Then you keep yourself always in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Our method is very simple. It doesn’t require much education or to become rich or become beautiful or become—to take birth in high family. No. In any condition. *Ahaituky apratihatā.* If you want to chant Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra, there is no impediment in the world. You can, walking on the street, you can chant Hare Kṛṣṇa. That will keep you purified.

On July 2, during your final *Śrīmad*-*Bhāgavatam* class in Denver (on 6.1.19), you mercifully said that those who are always thinking of Kṛṣṇa by chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa are safe. From this we understand that your instructions are our full shelter and security. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for this spiritual safety, along with which you give us the mercy to serve you properly.

Some of your final words of this class encouraged us to keep ourselves in this safe position of always serving Kṛṣṇa in various ways:

So if we go on with this habit, Kṛṣṇa consciousness, then our position is very secure, and keep yourself in that secure position rigidly. Then your life is successful. Thank you very much.

Thank *you* very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Of course, we cannot thank you enough. But we beg for your blessings so that we are always able to try to serve you with gratitude and that ultimately we may learn to serve you and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda with pure love. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your aspiring servants at New Badarikāśrama, the ISKCON temple in Denver, Colorado, USA.

## Newcastle (South Africa)

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most sincere and humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet, which are our only shelter in this Age of Kali.

Struggling to repay a debt that is long overdue, we shall try our best to ensure that our service brings nothing but pleasure to Your Divine Grace.

We offer my humble obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Śrī Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.

Taking the order of his guru upon his head, he became empowered by Nityānanda Prabhu to act as a śaktyāveśa avatāra. He distributed the Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra all over the Eastern and Western world, delivering and uplifting all fallen souls.

He is the best of millions of *jagad-gurus* because he is the personification of divine mercy. He has distributed the sweet nectar of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and the transcendental knowledge of *Bhagavad-gītā* all over the world.

He is constantly engaged in exclusive devotional service to Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, Śrīla *Rūpa* *Gosvāmī,* and Śrī *Gaurāṅga* Mahāprabhu. We offer our humble obeisances unto Śrīla Prabhupāda, who is like a bumblebee always tasting the nectar of the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Govinda.

\* \* \*

*Śrīla Prabhupāda*, words alone cannot express our gratitude, so this year we thought it best if we offer our service for your pleasure. Our goal is to promote Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and also to keep the devotees of your ISKCON properly motivated and nurtured. In planning this offering, we thought about your mission, your supermost ideals, and your achievements. Your mission is the mission of all *Gauḍīya* Vaiṣṇavas, and how you went about fulfilling that mission provides an example worthy of emulation. With the highest standards and sincere dedication you have made ISKCON.

Many societies are named after their founder or leader, but you, *Śrīla Prabhupāda*, shifted the focus from yourself to Kṛṣṇa. Your ideal is also the ideal of all *Gauḍīya* Vaiṣṇavas, the highest ideal.

We begin in this life with some *sukṛti* from our past life. Those born in India are accredited with great *sukṛti*. You once said that an ant in India has achieved a more fortunate birth than a human being in other countries. And around the same time you said that the rickshaw walla in Vṛndāvana is superior to the president of the United States. This is not hard to accept. We have met a few rickshaw wallas in Vṛndāvana, and if we have a chance to meet the president of the United States, I’m sure we’ll confirm your judgment.

So those born in India have some *sukṛti* and begin life with some advantage, while those born in foreign countries, like ourselves, have no *sukṛti* and start at the beginning. But regardless of where we begin, very few of us, if any, are born with high ideals. But *Gauḍīya* Vaiṣṇavism embodies an extremely high ideal, exemplified in the lives of great *ācāryas* since the time of Śrī *Caitanya* Mahāprabhu.

What you achieved in such a short time seems possible only in dreams. So many had tried but failed because they did not have the blessings of the *guru*-*paramparā*. What is a miracle? A miracle is something beyond our capabilities, something that can be done only by someone with superhuman ability. Once, *Śrīla Prabhupāda*, you said, “I achieved what I did only by divine power. The divine came down to me and worked through me.” You said Śrī *Nityānanda* Prabhu personally danced within you, lived within you. Śrī *Nityānanda* Prabhu lived within your body and danced a miracle for twelve years all over the world, fulfilling Lord Caitanya’s prediction: *pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi-grāma/ sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma.*

But how it as actually happened, we cannot comprehend. In every town and village of the world, the holy name of Kṛṣṇa is being sung—from Bosnia to the battlefields of Afghanistan, from Kazakhstan to Siberia, from Kwa Mashu to the streets of remote towns like Newcastle, Dundee, Glencoe, Dannahauser, and even Soweto. From the remote aborigine villages of Africa to Trafalgar Square in London to Times Square in New York—all around the world the name of Kṛṣṇa is being sung. “Hare Kṛṣṇa” has become a household word. Actually, of course, the name of Kṛṣṇa is not “a household word”: it is a divine sound, a sound that only you could have caused to vibrate in the hearts of millions throughout the world.

*Śrīla Prabhupāda,* on this most auspicious day please allow us fallen souls to try to serve your Society with sincerity, honesty, integrity, complete devotion, and strong conviction that the holy name will always be our only shelter.

Your most fallen servants at the ISKCON temple in Newcastle, South Africa.

## New Dvārākā

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

***“*****I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated, humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

On this 117th anniversary of your merciful appearance in this world, please also accept the following words of appreciation, inadequate and unrefined though they may be.

In a letter to Kṣīrodakaśāyī Dāsa, dated January 29th, 1973, you said: “These temples, they are just like oasis in the desert for the conditioned souls to quench the thirst of their desire for real happiness. So they must be organized as nicely as possible, and I know you have many talents which can be put to good use.” Here in New Dvārakā, the Los Angeles branch of your International Society for Krishna Consciousness, we are trying our level best to maintain the spiritual oasis you created here.

For your pleasure, New Dvārakā remains a perennial leader in book distribution in North America. This accomplishment is fueled by the strong desire of the local leaders and maintained by the staunch commitment of the resident devotees, ably assisted by congregation members. Though local political leaders are preventing devotees from distributing books at the Los Angeles International airport (LAX), the devotees remain undaunted and continue to find ways to increase book distribution.

You again compared your ISKCON centers to spiritual oases in the following statement:

You have written that your temple is like an oasis in the desert. Yes, it is a fact that our temples are providing shelter from the burning forest fires of material existence. It does not matter poor quarter or richer quarter, our temples are nothing to do with any material situation. They are situated in the spiritual sky, and as such they are the only alternative offering relief from the miseries of all kinds of material conditions of life. So encourage people to come and spend time with us in the temple and become purified from all contaminations and anxieties. Everyone is suffering from thirst for spiritual life, or Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and it is the temple which can offer them Kṛṣṇa consciousness to satisfy their thirsting. Regarding your temple schedule of study, it is all right, so continue your classes regularly and try to understand the philosophy as it is. That is required. \[Letter to Jagadīśa, 9 July 1970\]

Another noteworthy element of the oasis-like atmosphere of your New Dvārakā community is the high standard the devotees maintain in their worship of the Deities—Śrī Śrī Rukmiṇī-Dvārakādhīśa; Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā; and Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi. The local devotees, regular congregation, and virtual congregation (via Internet *darśana*) greatly appreciate the devotion, dedication, and talent of the fortunate *pūjārīs* who serve Their Lordships on a daily basis. During the many festivals observed throughout the year (e.g., Ratha-yātrā) the *pūjārīs* quite often go above and beyond their normal level of service to please the Deities.

In *Rāja-vidyā* you state:

There are nine different processes of devotional service, of which *śravaṇaṁ kīrtanam,* hearing and chanting, are the most important. *Kīrtanam* actually means “describing.” We can describe with music, words, pictures, etc. *Śravaṇam* goes hand in hand with *kīrtanam,* for unless we hear, we cannot describe. We don’t need any material qualifications in order to attain the Supreme. All we have to do is hear from authoritative sources and repeat accurately what we hear.

In a lecture on *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* given in New Vrindavan in June of 1969, you said:

So you haven’t got to make yourself very learned scholar or— Just like a postal peon. He’s not necessarily very learned scholar or even a very literary man. His only business is to take the envelope and put it in the right place. That’s all. Similarly, to spread this Kṛṣṇa consciousness, you haven’t got to become a very learned scholar or literary man. You have simply to understand— *Śravaṇaṁ kīrtanam*. You hear and spread this. People will be happy. *Akhila-bandha-muktaye*. They will be liberated from all problems. It is so nice thing.

Due in part to the fact that many New Dvārakā residents were initiated by you, classes on the philosophy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness maintain a high standard of fidelity to your teachings. We try our best to adhere to the principle of accurately repeating what we have heard from you. Many knowledgeable, sincere granddisciple class-givers are also on the speaking roster. New Dvārakā is also blessed with opportunities to hear from the many senior devotees who regularly visit.

You always urged your disciples and followers to make people fortunate by giving them opportunities to taste *kṛṣṇa*-*prasādam*. In a letter written to Girirāja Prabhu in April 1972 you said:

This prasadam distribution item is very, very important, and it is so much pleasing to me that in the beginning so many children are coming for taking prasadam, and that supply of rice had come automatically. Please continue this program rigidly. By prasadam distribution you will do service to the Lord, and the public will be pleased upon you as well. Gradually increase the number of guests, and don’t deny anyone. Continue in this way: whatever is there, distribute equally—it may not be very sumptuous, but no one should be denied equal portion.

By the mercy of the Deities, here in New Dvārakā there is never any shortage of *prasādam*. Temple devotees and regular guests are satisfied honoring the Deities’ *prasādam,* and our Govinda’s Natural Foods restaurant has a steady clientele of customers who appreciate the simple but tasty food and the peaceful spiritual atmosphere.

In a letter to Sudāmā Prabhu written in November 1972, you emphasized the importance of *harināma* and book distribution:

Yes, we shall base our preaching work on Lord Caitanya’s formula: sankirtana. After all, He is God, and if He recommends, what is the question of failure? That is not possible. If we simply stick to this program as I have done it since the beginning, namely, kirtana, preaching, kirtana, distribution of prasadam—if you do like this only that will be sufficient. Of course, you may make the kirtana as nice as possible, with dancing, many khols, karatalas, conches, and if you preach sincerely, anyone will listen and become convinced. The potency of Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement does not come from some outward showing, no, it is the transcendental sound vibration of Hare Krishna mantra and the words of *Bhagavad-Gita*, *Śrīmad-Bhagavatam*, like that.

We pray that you will continue to shower your blessings on the New Dvārakā community so that we may please you by sticking to the programs that you initiated and that make this community a spiritual oasis.

Your servants in New Dvārakā, Los Angeles.

## New Gayā (Tokyo)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Dear beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

We are not qualified to glorify Your Divine Grace. At the same time, we owe an infinite debt of gratitude to you and should open our heart and express it. Since you are always merciful toward the fallen souls, we are taking this opportunity to share some realizations of your presence with us.

Thank you very much for coming to this world and showering your blessings on us. Because of your mercy we have an opportunity to do some insignificant service in the mission of Lord *Caitanya*. We are trying, by your mercy, to follow your instructions.

Last year saw many changes in ISKCON Japan, both positive and negative. We experienced some tribulation when several devotees left the Japan *yātrā* and went to serve your mission elsewhere. This change inspired other devotees to take up leadership positions here to serve your mission. We’ve experienced that it is a challenge to keep devotees faithful and focused in this place, where the material energy seems to have her headquarters. We have tried our best in to serve the devotees, but only you can help us succeed.

You have set a very high standard of devotional service. We have to admit that we are struggling to follow the minimum standards. At the same time, you are very accommodative to one and all, without any discrimination of any sort.

You told your leaders to manage ISKCON properly or everything would be finished. We are trying to manage, but we are struggling. We beg your mercy so that we can strike the proper balance between management on the one had and *harināma,* book distribution, and preaching on the other.

We have read and heard from senior devotees how you single-handedly traveled to the West and established ISKCON. Please give us the strength and courage to be fearless in preaching with constant determination.

Please give us the strength to tolerate the disturbances in preaching and managing the temple without deviating from the basic principles. We may have committed many offenses against devotees and at your lotus feet. Please forgive us. We can see your mercy flowing in many situations when we are helpless.

We sometimes wonder what our lives would have been like without your teachings. At the very least we would have continued to look in vain for answers to the plethora of questions that keep coming to our relentless mind. Without your teachings, every moment we would have struggled choose between sense gratification and the soul. We are infinitely indebted to Your Divine Grace for imparting to us your divine teachings through your books, your disciples, and our godbrothers and -sisters. We strongly believe that for a very long time your divine teachings will continue to change the lives of many fallen souls like us by awakening love of Godhead in their hearts.

In this Kali-yuga, where *bhakti* is the only way for spirituality, you underwent all pains to spread *harināma* and taught us about real *bhakti* and genuine spirituality. Your books are pure and reveal the truth of *bhakti* as it is, without any changes. Our humble request is that you keep us engaged in Śrī *Caitanya* Mahāprabhu’s mission in Japan, though it is one of the most challenging countries in which to perform *harināma*. Your books are our guidelines and real inspiration.

Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for all that you have given us. Your glories are unfathomable.

Your servants at ISKCON New Gayā, Tokyo, Japan.

## New Gokula (Australia)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept this very humble homage to Your divine Grace. I just found out that today is the deadline for offerings.

We want to be counted as one of your branches despite our relative insignificance.

**To Our Beloved Guide**

Your infallible directions are our only guiding light.

Those who explain your words in a way that tallies with the obvious conclusions established in your teachings are our worthy authorities.

Your pre-eminence is the mooring of our loyalty. And those who reinforce our faith in your divinity are our worshipable mentors.

Your wonderful optimism for the establishment of a strong worldwide network of Kṛṣṇa conscious communities is our great hope. And those who embody your ambitious plans are our field marshals

Your innocent mirth is the solace of world-weary souls,  
Those who share spiritual joy are your blessed heirs.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the words of your followers in this Vyāsa-pūjā book will be spiritual sustenance for many souls over the coming year, until fresher and heightened appreciations continue to supersede the previous revelations of inspiration and gratitude for the greatness of Your Divine Grace.

We simply stand in great awe of your virtues—your profound intelligence, your insuperable achievements, and especially your being forever approachable to all of us who resort to your lotus feet as the ultimate shelter for getting free from *māyā*.

Please bless this community so we may prosper under your benevolence

*Y*our servants at the New Gokula Farm, Millfield, Australia.  
*(*written by Kāliya Kṛṣṇa Dāsa)

## New Gokula (Vancouver)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. Please accept these words we have written for you on this most auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā.

Every day here at ISKCON Vancouver we take *darśana* of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā–Madana-mohana and relish the association of wonderful devotees, all chanting and dancing together. We may think that this is just another day in our advancement in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. But this is not “just another day,” and no day is “just another day” since you came to remind us of what we had forgotten: who we really are and what our relationship with Lord Kṛṣṇa is.

None of this would be happening if it were not for you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You have shown us, with love, patience and compassion, how we can get out of this miserable material existence and return home to the spiritual world. Without you there would be no temple, no Deities, no devotees, no *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and no *sādhana* of any kind for us.

Our lives have been transformed by your presence, your wisdom, your words, and your example, and by your causeless mercy we have been given the precious gift of transcendental knowledge. There is no way we can even begin to repay you for your kindness to us, but we pray that we—your sons and daughters and grandsons and granddaughters—may spend every moment of every extraordinary day striving to serve you in a way that pleases you. May we never forget your dedication and service to the instruction of your Guru Mahārāja, nor the devotion that brought you to us here in Canada. With your ever-present encouragement, may we continually progress in our Kṛṣṇa consciousness and spend every extraordinary day here at New Gokula-dhāma taking *darśana* of Śrī Śrī Rādhā–Madana-mohana and dancing and chanting in the association of devotees.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your commitment to bringing the effulgent light of transcendental knowledge and bliss into our dark and dreary lives. On this, the anniversary of your glorious appearance in this world, we bow down in gratitude to you and offer our most sincere heartfelt love and devotion.

Your servants at New Gokula-dhāma, Vancouver, Canada.  
(written by Padyāvalī Devī Dāsī and Bhavāni Devī Dāsī)

## New Govardhana (San Diego)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

I am honored to be writing this offering on behalf of the New Govardhana temple community. By your mercy alone, I grew up with San Diego’s Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Giridhārī as my revered Lords, their devotees as my extended family, and the temple as my sanctuary and home away from home.

Words fail me as I attempt to describe the debt we all feel for your gift of pure *bhakti.* You revealed the path to *kṛṣṇa-prema* in the most pleasing, accessible manner, showing by example how the simple process of chanting, dancing, and feasting will bring us to ecstatic perfection, What could be more simple and sublime? And because of that unprecedented, pristine gift, it is impossible to ever achieve a sufficient pinnacle of gratitude and love for you. I beg at your lotus feet that you accept this meager attempt to praise you and thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

My family arrived in San Diego in the fall of 1969 with no idea that, although we came to America in search of material fortune and were far from India, it is here that we would find the most valuable spiritual jewel as a manifestation of your causeless mercy. That year, on our trek toward San Diego, we visited the Los Angeles temple and were told what would prove to be life-changing news, i.e., that a temple was opening in San Diego.

The San Diego temple, New Govardhana, is the fountain of spiritual nectar, where my family and innumerable others found true fortune in the form of the beautiful Deities, your life-changing books, and a family of compassionate devotees. I remember how as a small child I visited the small house that was the first San Diego temple. And as I grew older I was blessed to watch first-hand the manifestation of your powerful mercy in the blossoming lotus of ISKCON San Diego—steadily transforming, despite many impediments, into an increasingly vibrant spiritual community.

By emulating your compassion, love, and tolerance, we are blessed to widely distribute Lord Caitanya’s transcendental message in innovative ways: from the Kṛṣṇa Lounge to the Kṛṣṇa Dome to Friday night candlelight *kīrtanas,* from Bhakti Chakra groups to family Vaiṣṇava movie nights to our Christmas *prasādam* festival and picnics in the park. To serve the Deities we have even started a small organic farm with two cows. You have taught us that everything must be dovetailed in Kṛṣṇa’s service, and the devotees of New Govardhana have taken your words to heart, making them a reality. All these venues not only bring the *mahā-mantra* and your teachings to new devotees but also continually nourish the devotional creepers of our whole community.

You saved us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and the only way we can attempt to repay such a debt is to pass on this wonderful Kṛṣṇa conscious process to others. Please allow us to continue expanding our San Diego temple *āśrama* and *gṛhastha* community as our offering to your compassionate and loving lotus feet. Bless us all so we may faithfully fulfill Lord Caitanya’s desire that the *mahā-mantra* be loudly chanted in every town and village. And most of all, let us eternally remember you, praise you, serve you, and remain under the loving umbrella of your ISKCON family, sitting at the lotus feet of Lord *Caitanya* and Lord *Nityānanda*.

Please accept our most humble and grateful obeisances.

Your grateful, loving followers at New Govardhana, ISKCON’s devotee community in San Diego, California, USA.  
(written by Phaṇeśvarī Devī Dāsī)

## New Hastināpura

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you!

Often I envision you in your room on the *Jaladuta*—your body at an advanced age, your health unstable, experiencing the devastating pain of heart attacks while in the bowels of a cargo ship on the open sea, having left behind your beloved Vṛndāvana for the unknown of the very wild West. In the throes of this external crisis you write what many find your most inspiring and illuminating words. Words in which you sweetly, beautifully, and simply express your profound humility, infinite compassion, and the perfection of loving devotion. You cried, “Make me dance, make me dance, O Lord, make me dance as You like!”

Here at our temple we have devotees from all over the world, representing six continents. All ages, colors, and walks of life. On this wonderfully auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā we are gathered together in front of your *mūrti*. Overwhelmed by your sacrifices to save our lowly and undeserving selves, bewildered by our great fortune to have the priceless gift of your divine association, we are all inspired to dance and cry.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, here at our temple you have inspired so many dedicated devotees to dance in lovely ways. There are book distribution campaigns, college preaching programs, interfaith communication and preaching, growing Nāma-haṭṭa programs, *prasādam* distribution, and many other offerings. We pray for your blessings so these efforts enjoy wider participation, support, and expansion.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, to the whole world you have distributed the holy name, along with the essence of Vedic knowledge in your books. You have built a house the whole world can live in while tasting the *prasādam* of transcendental knowledge via your ISKCON. All over the world you have inspired the creation of temples and preaching centers. Who can count your unlimited mercy and blessings? But for us, the sweetest fruit from the vast forests of your accomplishments are your nectarean sons and daughters. They are living, moving manifestations of your purports. When we see their complete surrender to you, our faith is inspired. With their pioneering spirit, abandoning all consideration of material prospects, they live and breathe for you. Their words and deeds give us a glimpse of real love. They give us the hope of experiencing real love. They give us the purification and immense happiness of associating with real lovers of Kṛṣṇa. How could we live in this world without them?

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you. We are eternally grateful. We pray to you for the unfathomable blessing to continue dancing in your service and the service of your moonlike sons and daughters. Thus our otherwise pitiable lives will have meaning.

Your followers in New Hastināpura Dhāma, ISKCON of Washington, D.C., USA.  
(written by Kuntī Devī Dāsī)

## New Navadvīpa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you on this most auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā!

It is hard to imagine where we would be now if it weren’t for your causeless mercy. Following the instruction of your Guru Mahārāja, you came to the West to save the fallen conditioned souls. How can we sufficiently thank you for this eternal gift of love? We can only pray that we may follow in your footsteps, continuing the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and spreading the chanting of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa.

In New Navadvīpa we are especially fortunate to be a *tīrtha,* a place you visited six times. To be able to walk in your footsteps or sit with you in your room and chant our *japa—*what a treasure! So many wonderful guests come to your temple here and are able to visit with you in your quarters and hear the amazing story of your journey to America at the age of seventy, and of how in only twelve years you circled the globe so many times, opened temples around the world, and translated numerous volumes of the Vedic literature. Each guest leaves with a sense of peace and of newfound respect for your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Devotees who never had the good fortune to personally associate with you can spend time in your presence and even take a morning walk with you on Magic Island.

You have given us such a legacy. You instructed the devotees here how to make the first Pañca-tattva Deities in the movement and personally installed them. In this way you gave us the opportunity to always take shelter of the mercy of their lotus feet. The first Tulasī garden in America flourished here in the Hawaiian Islands. We have fourth-generation devotees growing up under the banyan tree on your property here, chanting and dancing in front of Śrī Pañca-tattva, and this year four of our youth took their vows of initiation.

In service to you we have a wonderful *prasādam* distribution program. At the temple restaurant we get visitors from all walks of life and many different cities, and on the campus of the University of Hawaii we have professors and students eagerly lining up to taste a plate of *kṛṣṇa*-*prasādam.* Many students volunteer to help serve out, thus engaging in devotional service.

Our Jagannātha Ratha-yātrā processions, as well as our *mahā-harināma* *saṅkīrtana* parties, chant and dance their way along the ocean on the world-famous Waikiki Beach. People from all over the world, even from places where they may not of heard of Kṛṣṇa before, can hear the chanting of the *mahā-mantra*. They are all enlivened by the devotees’ chanting, and many shoot videos with their smartphones to share with friends and family at home. In this way we are helping fulfill Lord Caitanya’s prediction that His name would be heard and chanted in every town and village.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we take shelter of your lotus feet and constantly pray that by your mercy and guidance we may continue to serve you always in spreading this Hare Kṛṣṇa movement.

Your eternal servants at New Navadvīpa Dhāma, Honolulu, Hawaii.  
(written by Madana Sundarī Devī Dāsī)

## New Navadvīpa (Kiev)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our most humble obeisances at the holy dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

When I was asked to write an offering to you on behalf of our Kiev temple—New Navadvīpa Mandir—I felt honored and also grateful to the Vaiṣṇavas for giving me the opportunity to render such a rare service, although I felt completely unqualified. I’m feeling gratitude for the trust they have in me.

Why do I mention that? Because this is exactly why we are all grateful to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. From all the accounts of your *līlā,* accounts which are now available by the inconceivable mercy of those great souls who had direct association with you, I can tell that one of the most prominent qualities of Your Divine Grace, which attracted everyone, was that you always placed your trust in your disciples, even though they were not qualified. And that trust would empower and encourage them to do their best in fulfilling your desires. That was real preaching—to give hope to the hopless. That was actual love. And that was real “brāhmaṇism.” In a lecture you gave on *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.9.49 in Māyāpur on 15 June 1973, you described real “brāhmaṇism”: “That is brāhmaṇism. *Brāhmaṇa* means liberal, and just the opposite word of *brāhmaṇa* is *kṛpaṇa,* miser. A *brāhmaṇa* should give chance to everyone, even to the *pāpa-yoni,* how to become *brāhmaṇa*. That is real *brahmana*.”

In this regard, recently I spoke with a devotee friend about his realizations from visiting Śrī Māyāpur *dhāma* and Śrī Vṛndāvana *dhāma* (especially your rooms at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Dāmodara temple). He told me that what had struck him the most was how it is possible for us, your granddisciples, to feel so close to you, how you are much more dear to us than any of our material relatives or friends. I wondered why that is so, and concluded that because you trust us despite all our mistakes, because you can always understand us while always remaining in your exalted position, never compromising your principles, we feel you to be our best and closest friend. And we are ready to accept from you even the heaviest words, for we understand that those words are the words of love. You’re like a mother who always sees the best in her child, and because of that vision the child can grow and develop excellent qualities.

Now your society is growing tremendously due to this love of yours. And this growing ISKCON family inspires even more feelings of gratitude toward you. Today people are not united anymore. It is so rare to belong to a family whose members have nice relationships among one another. This is especially rare in the West, where everything is so impersonal. That’s why—especially at big festivals like Māyāpur—to see so many devotees from such varied backgrounds united in one big family, to feel this unity, to chant the holy names while feeling these friendly relationships with such a variety of devotees—this is such a great gift and a treasure you have given us.

And I would like to share my appreciation for one more gift of yours. Yielding to the friendly insistence of a senior godbrother and –sister of mine, I went to take some medical treatment from a devotee *mātājī* who is a highly qualified medical doctor. After the treatment, I said to her, “*Mātājī,* I’m so grateful to you for all the effort and time you put in for my treatment—”. But as I was saying this she just started crying. Then she said, “Actually, thank you very much for giving me the opportunity of service. It is I who am grateful for this.” I was moved so much by such sincerity and purity.

Later on and I thought that this mood of unconditional and unselfish devotional service, which you brought to us directly from Goloka, is the rarest, most precious thing in this world. We are trying to cultivate this mood of service, in which everything we do for Kṛṣṇa and His devotees we consider a great opportunity and therefore in itself the greatest reward we can dream of. But some devotees somehow imbibe this mood from the very beginning of their devotional lives, and therefore they are the most fortunate souls. I’m so grateful to you for showing us this example, and I’m so grateful to you for showing us this mood through your faithful followers.

All of these gifts I have mentioned (and there are many more) give meaning to our lives—they give us goals to live for and to die for. Having received all these most precious gifts from you, I feel that my most urgent duty is to share this treasure with others.

Your servants at New Navadvīpa Mandir, Kiev, Ukraine.  
(written by Dāmodara Caraṇa Dāsa)

## New Nīlācala (Guadalajara)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, after one of your lectures in Mexico City in 1975, a devotee asked you, “*Śrīla Prabhupāda*, why are we so fortunate to have such a nice spiritual master like you, while others have bogus spiritual masters. Is this due to our *karma* or to your pure mercy?”

You answered:

No, that you can think—your spiritual master is nice, others bogus—but they do not think like that. They will think their spiritual master is good, your spiritual master is bogus. But there is standard who is spiritual master. Spiritual master means who is the best servant of God. One who does not agree to the existence of God, he is bogus because he’s not bona fide, *mūḍhā*. One who does not accept the existence of God, he is *mūḍhā*. He’s a rascal. A rascal cannot become spiritual master.

The spiritual master is he, *samit-pāṇiḥ śrotriyaṁ brahma-niṣṭham,* one who is exclusively servant of God, he is spiritual master. Otherwise anyone will come and say, “I am spiritual master.” Anyone will come say, “I am incarnation of God.” So there is standard. That we have to find out.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you always told everybody that the spiritual master is an *ācārya, one who* teaches by his example, and you showed by your example how to execute the nine processes of devotional service. Everyone who claims to be a bona fide follower of yours must follow your example.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please never stop giving your causeless mercy to us fallen devotees at New Nīlācala Mandira.

Your devotees at New Nīlācala Mandira, Guadalajara, Mexico.  
(written by Premānanda Dāsa)

## New Pānihāṭi Dhāma

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

This year, before attending the Gaura Pūrṇimā festival in Śrīdhām Māyāpur, I visited Pānihāṭi in West Bengal for the first time. There your granddisciple Naru Gopāl Dāsa took a small group of devotees on *parikramā* of that holy place of Lord Nityānanda’s pastimes. I prayed at the various holy sites for Lord *Nityānanda* to empower your followers in New Pānihāṭi Dhāma (Atlanta) for success in spreading the *saṅkīrtana* movement. It is a difficult task.

Lord Nityānanda’s specialty was saving the Jagāis and Mādhāis of the world. Lord *Nityānanda* even took in those few devotees who fell from Lord Caitanya’s grace and engaged them in the *saṅkīrtana* movement. You have done the same here in Atlanta, at your New Pānihāṭi Dhāma.

Your servants in Atlanta have trained some new young Western devotees, who are taking up book distribution, various temple services, and other preaching activities. We are making a some progress, though it is quite meager compared with that of your *yātrās* in India and Russia. Just how meager was evident when I attended the Gaura Pūrṇimā festival in Māyāpur this year. The American contingent is tiny compared to Russians and Indians. In 1977, practically all attendees were Americans.

We are determined to continue the fight in Kali’s *dhāma,* the USA. We shouldn’t expect easy victory. We pray that you empower us so that we can glorify you by delivering the lost souls in Atlanta. We pray that Lord *Nityānanda* also empower us to please you by increasing the members of the *saṅkīrtana* movement in Atlanta.

Your servants at New Pānihāṭi Dhāma, ISKCON’s temple in Atlanta, Georgia, USA.  
(written by Śikhi Māhiti Dāsa)

## New Ramaṇa Reti Dhāma

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

You are carrying the sacred order (*ājñāya*) of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, the order that, when embraced, rescues us from a life of sin, protects us from sinful reactions, and gives us the real goal of life (*sādhya*) and the means to attain it (*sādhana)*. That order affords us an alternative to existing on the mental platform.

Out of compassion you conveyed to us the Lord’s words (*śabda brahma*) at very high personal risk, with colossal endeavor, and with practically no resources, facilities, or help from anyone else. You knew that the Truth was dynamically represented by the words passed down in disciplic succession, and that in those words lay solace and resolution of all problems for all the world. You knew this because you were living it yourself and you had heard *śabda brahma* with total faith.

And as you pass on this life-giving transcendental perspective, you are present in those instructions. That is where we can find you—in your *vāṇī* (speech). We cannot naively imagine that there is another way to approach you, to feel your reciprocation and your mercy, or to please you. To be pulled out of the well, we must grab the rope.

In this dark age, where and what is religion? That question is answered by the Supreme Personality of Godhead Kṛṣṇa, who came as Śrī *Gaurāṅga* Mahāprabhu, the inconceivably merciful one, and established His *saṅkīrtana* movement. His movement provides us the only way to act that is not impelled by the modes of nature and the universal law of *karma*. We live, act, eat, sing, work, and preach for you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for Kṛṣṇa.

Whether we live in the temple or in a *gṛha*, our life revolves around service to the Deity. As for our contribution to society, you have asked us to do as you did and introduce Kṛṣṇa consciousness as widely as possible; ISKCON’s function is brahminical.

You ordered us to chant sixteen rounds daily, to study your books, and to refrain from intoxicants, meat-eating, illicit sex, and gambling. You ordered us to co-operate within ISKCON to fulfill the desires of the Lord. You absolutely wanted us to preach. You spoke for the Lord, who is in all our hearts, who is our eternal, intimate, well-wishing friend. And we can associate with you and Him *if we do what you told us to do.* There is no shortcut, no alternative.

We at the New Ramaṇa Reti community are praying that you bless our humble attempt to maintain a transcendental oasis for those who want to practice Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and to expand the influence of ISKCON in northern Florida.

We have:

• a strong morning program 365 days a year

• vigorous book distribution

• six annual Ratha-yātrā festivals

• cow protection

• extensive vegetable production

• two Food for Life programs

• wonderful, ebullient Vaiṣṇava festivals

• an expertly managed Bhaktivedanta Academy gurukula

• an active prison preaching team

• bhajana and study groups in homes every evening of the week

• an annual Festival of the Holy Name

• weekly harināma saṅkīrtana parties

Plans are under way to:

• expand our guest facilities

• hold seminars on Kṛṣṇa consciousness and Vedic culture

• create an Eco Teaching Farm

• increase book distribution

• build a larger temple complex

By our constitution as *jīvas* (souls) we must serve. We choose to serve you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. That is the highest service, the topmost worship, the secret of attaining liberation and love of God. We serve you because it was *you* who crossed ocean to save us from a horrible fate. We choose to serve you because you are the very best, without comparison (*na tat-samaś cābhyadhikaś ca dṛśyate*). This has been categorically, irrefutably proven for all time by the Vaiṣṇava litmus test: seeing how many devotees one has made.

And what is ISKCON? It is you, an expansion of you—of your ideas, your desires, your plans.

What keeps it so? Its members’ adherence to your orders.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this most holy, auspicious, blessed anniversary of your appearance in the world, we humbly beseech you with folded hands to give us the intelligence, the faith, the courage, the sincerity, and everything else required to be always driven, protected, and purified by your divine orders.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants at New Ramaṇa Reti, ISKCON’s rural community in Alachua, Florida, USA.

## New Remuṇā Dhāma

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for delivering the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu to the entire world!
