We hear so often of the pleasure experienced by devotees when they saw you smile at the book distribution reports. On this, your appearance day, we hope we can bring a smile to your face by reporting our book scores.

Here in New Remuṇā Dhāma we are trying to constantly remember your instructions and carry them out. On January 8th, 1974 you wrote to Jagadīśa Dāsa, then GBC for Canada:

Your emphasis on book distribution is quite right. I can understand in the northern centers it is very cold now, and yet you report they are still going out for sankirtana party. Please convey that I extend all blessings to those boys and girls who are trying to distribute our books even under difficult conditions.

By your inspiration, in 2012 we distributed over 40,000 books, and we are on our way to distributing even more in 2013.

This is a very small accomplishment compared to your service to your Guru Mahārāja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Prabhupāda. Every day we hear of people performing some welfare activity for the so-called benefit of mankind, but the results of this type of welfare work are temporary: ultimately they leave everyone still suffering from the fourfold miseries of birth, death, old age, and disease.

When you boarded the *Jaladuta* to leave India to come to the West, at the time it seemed an insignificant event. But we now realize that it was the most effective and compassionate action in the history of mankind. Never before has one action resulted in so many people being freed from the fourfold miseries and, even more importantly, becoming intimately aware of their relationship with Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead. This transcendental welfare work is your life and soul. Our desire is that it also become our life and soul. Please continue to engage us in your service, life after life, for therein lies our perfection.

On this most auspicious anniversary of your appearance, we devotees of New Remuṇā Dhāma pray that we can always cooperate in helping to spread the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu to every town and village.

Your eternal servants at New Remuṇā Dhāma, ISKCON’s temple in Toronto, Canada.

## New Tālavana Dhāma

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your transcendental lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Service! May we forever remain fixed in your devotional service.

A person who can lead others out of darkness, back to home, back to Godhead, is actually a *mahātmā,* or great soul. There have been many religious, political, economic, and social leaders in recorded history, but none match your qualifications. Surrendering utterly to the directions given to you by Lord Kṛṣṇa’s bona fide representative in an authorized line of disciple succession, you are rescuing humanity from the dangerous pitfalls of Kali-yuga. Your words give everyone a way out of darkness and clearly show the road back home, back to Godhead.

We can never repay you for showing us the way out of darkness and leading us along that road. Yet all you ask from us is to help others along the path. At a time when everyone tries to earn a living or finance a project by exploiting others, you set an example of purity by trying to help others and not charging for your services. As an *ācārya,* or holy teacher, you gave mercy to all and allowed people to give the fruits of their labor to serve guru and *Gaurāṅga*. Thus you engaged the poor conditioned souls in Lord *Gaurāṅga*’s transcendental *saṅkīrtana* movement, the dharma for the age. Hence in a time of capitalism and wage-earners you avoided personal profit and utilized all contributions to push on the stated missions of your nonprofit organizations, the International Society for Krishna Consciousness and the Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, to satisfy your Guru Mahārāja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Prabhupāda.

You spent countless hours training your disciples to follow in your footsteps so the mission would continue after you left this world. You created ISKCON, the BBT, and the BI to carry on this most important work, and you took the trouble to put in place the management structure to oversee them. You were not satisfied going back home to Godhead alone or with a few followers. You wanted to make it possible for everyone to do so. Thus you poured out blood, sweat, and tears to translate the most important transcendental scriptures into common English, with elaborate purports to explain the text. You put your organizational ideas down in black and white to manage your institution, and you cajoled, inspired, and directed your disciples, public leaders, and people in general. You are a modern saint to be followed by anyone aspiring to transcend the cycle of birth and death.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, bless us so that we may rub our noses in the dust of your lotus feet. Please let us always serve you, and do not hesitate to correct us and chastise us. Please ask the Paramātmā to enlighten our humble selves with transcendental directions, and bless us with devotion for you. Please help us find our way out of illusion and become more fixed in the science of devotion so that we may ultimately taste the divine mellows of *bhāva* and *prema,* ecstatic love of Godhead. We are prepared to continue being patient yet enthusiastic disciples. Let us always serve your mission despite our lack of qualification.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for everything, for without you we would not know the Absolute Truth, Bhagavān Śrī Kṛṣṇa, nor our very selves. You are responsible for our good fortune, and therefore we should surrender unto your lotus feet with submission, love, and devotion.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, all glories to your transcendental loving devotional service!

*Y*our servants at New Tālavana Dhāma, ISKCON’s rural community in Mississippi, and at ISKCON of New Orleans, Louisiana, USA.  
*(*written by Yogīndra Vandana Dāsa)

## New Varṣāṇā

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances. All glories unto you!

Sometimes while reading your books, listening to your lectures or sweet singing, or hearing a *kīrtana* in the temple and observing the wonderful service offered by your devotees, I am simply astounded by what you have done for us.

You have given us everything we need to re-establish our Kṛṣṇa consciousness—*kīrtana,* *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* *Bhagavad-gītā* *As It Is, Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* *prasādam,* beautiful Deities, sweet association, and amazing festivals.

Despite the loud protests of our ever-restless minds, what else do we need? We will achieve perfection in our lives simply by following your instructions and remaining steadfastly faithful to your ISKCON.

Your ISKCON is the vehicle for delivering the citizens of Kali-yuga, and you are and always will be its transcendental driver. Over the next ten thousand years ISKCON will grow and grow, and we will see many gurus come and go. But you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, will always be our *ācārya,* our loving father, grandfather, and great-grandfather, our dearmost friend and ever well-wisher.

Your servants at New Varṣāṇā, ISKCON’s rural community near Auckland, New Zealand.

## New Vraja Dhāma

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our respectful obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Pilgrims arriving in New Vraja-dhāma—devotees and not-yet-devotee tourists—first enter the gate of Krishna Valley and then start their walk in the earthly replica of the spiritual world on the Śrīla Prabhupāda promenade.

We receive twenty thousand guests yearly, and every year they get to admire ever more petals of Vraja-dhāma’s constantly opening lotus. This year we are celebrating the twentieth anniversary of Vraja-dhāma’s founding, and therefore we are offering them even more surprises. They can leave their cars in a larger parking lot and get information about the sites of interest in more convenient ways. At the start of their excursion they begin to taste the sweetness of spiritual life through the words of your books and some *prasādam*. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, as noted above, our pilgrims take their first step on their spiritual journey on the walkway named after you. Please allow us, your servants, to offer this year’s newly planted and blossoming flowers to your lotus feet. We would like to fill the hearts of more and more visiting souls with the fragrance of these flowers as we guide them through the holy places of New Vraja-dhāma.

With the help of diligent oxen, *gopas* are just now reaping the fragrant grass by the Prabhupāda promenade running on the bank of the river Yamunā. As we turn toward the *gurukula* we can hear the jolly sound of devotee children rehearsing a drama based on stories from the newly published storybooks. The cows and oxen in the *gośālā* are eager to tell the four new calves to be born this year the nicest memories of their year: the First Pasture Festival, where they could preach about cow protection, and the First-Grain Harvesting Festival, where participants had a chance to think about what the real wealth in their life was.

Leaving Nandagrāma for Varṣāṇā, one sees Govardhana’s orchards in the distance, full of trees laden with delicious fruits throughout the summer because of having been intoxicated by the Fruit Festival. The potato and pumpkin plantations next to the orchards, jealous of the fruit trees, are eagerly waiting for autumn so that they can also enjoy our celebration of their harvest with colorful festivals.

From Varṣāṇā we go through *Gaurāṅga* Square to Tri Bhuvana Sundara, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara’s newly renovated temple. During the three-day event called Open Days, the most beautiful Deities in the three worlds enchanted thousands of people daily with their beauty and the power of Their holy names.

Our next stop is Govinda’s restaurant, where one can taste a variety of *prasādam* prepared from the produce of our self-sufficient farm. Those who are curious can learn the secrets of self-sufficient farming from monthly cooking courses, courses about organic gardening, and the three-day Eco Valley conference. And those who want to know about the *varṇāśrama* society that is the motor behind the economic success of New Vraja-dhāma can take part in the everyday life of the devotee community through the weekly programs of the Councilor System, which is based on personal relationships and care.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for engaging us in your service by letting us help to fulfill your mission under the leadership of your loyal disciple His Holiness Śivarāma Swami. This service gives meaning and vital force to our lives. As we work to fulfill ISKCON’s purposes under your leadership—by systematically propagating spiritual knowledge based on the *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad*-*Bhāgavatam* and the chanting of the holy names, by publishing Kṛṣṇa conscious books, by engaging the members of the community in the service of *Rādhe*-Śyāma, by leading a simple, self-sufficient life, and by revealing the pastime places of the Divine Couple—New Vraja-dhāma’s lotus flower opens ever-newer fragrant petals. We offer these petals to your lotus feet so that more and more fallen souls may take to your service and you can lead them to the heart of Rādhe-Śyāma on the promenade named after you.

Your servants at New Vraja-dhāma, ISKCON’s rural community in Hungary.  
(written by Aṣṭa Sakhī Devī Dāsī)

## New Vrindaban

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Recently we held a series of meetings here to study your letters concerning New Vrindaban. In those letters you offered many instructions to the devotees for developing a Kṛṣṇa conscious community. From the letters we ascertained five major goals for our community to focus on. Four of them you addressed early on: cow protection, self-sufficiency, replicating Vṛndāvana (making a place of pilgrimage), and spiritual education.

In regards to the cows, you explained:

Krishna, by His practical example, taught us to give all protection to the cows, and that should be the main business of New Vrindaban. \[Letter to Hayagrīva, 14 June 68\]

You urged us to “live there self independently, simply raising cows, grains, fruits, and flowers” (12 January 69) and “establish a local self-governing village and show all the world a practical example of spiritual life as Krishna Himself exhibited in Vrindaban” (27 July 73) and promote “plain living and high thinking.” (14 June 68)

As for a place of pilgrimage, you asked us to “try your best to construct an exact duplicate of Vrindaban,” and you added that “that will give me the highest pleasure.” (18 February 70)

And early on, you also suggested a “school for educating brahmanas and Vaisnavas.” (12 January 69) And again, “Another important thing is our theistic school in New Vrindaban.” (9 March 70)

But a later letter would reveal the fifth goal, and the actual intent of the four above goals, and indeed of all the activities in New Vrindaban. This was perhaps your most sublime instruction for the devotees here:

The cows, the trees, the cowherd men and Gopis, their chief engagement was loving Krishna, and in New Vrindaban we want to create this atmosphere and thereby show the whole world how practical and sublime our movement is. \[Letter to Kīrtanānanda, 27 July 1973\]

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are like Kṛṣṇa’s great commander, strategizing and maneuvering forces into place. You are like a great director, giving us direction on how Lord Caitanya’s drama should unfold. You are like a great professor or lawyer, standing before a class or court of law arguing your case, offering documentation and pronouncements from Kṛṣṇa and the previous *ācāryas*.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are like a magnificent rain cloud, constantly showering down upon us the mercy of your instructions. Our prayer is that we may always experience the shower of your supreme kindness by taking up your instructions wholeheartedly. O Prabhupāda, allow us to always remember why we took shelter of your lotus feet and of your divine movement. Allow us to always remain grateful in our service to guru and Kṛṣṇa. Allow us to always appreciate and serve your devotees. And allow us to always chant Kṛṣṇa’s holy name attentively and without offense.

Here in New Vrindavan you have given us a lifetime of service—actually, many lifetimes. And this, O dear Prabhupāda, is our privilege. You have provided us with a life of privilege. For what could be a greater joy, honor, and blessing than serving the mission of Your Divine Grace?

Your servants at New Vrindaban, ISKCON’s rural community in West Virginia, USA.  
(written by Saṅkīrtana Dāsa)

## New York City (Brooklyn)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

*nāhaṁ vipro na ca nara-patir nāpi vaiśyo na śūdro*  
*nāhaṁ varṇī na ca gṛha-patir no vanastho yatir vā*  
*kintu prodyan-nikhila-paramānanda-pūrnāmṛtābdher*  
*gopī-bhartuḥ pada-kamalayor dāsa-dāsānudāsaḥ*

**I am not a** ***brāhmaṇa,*** **I am not a** ***kṣatriya,*** **I am not a** ***vaiśya*** **or a** ***śūdra*****. Nor am I a** ***brahmacārī,*** **a householder, a** ***vānaprastha,*** **or a** ***sannyāsī*****. I identify Myself only as the servant of the servant of the servant of the lotus feet of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the maintainer of the** ***gopīs*****. He is like an ocean of nectar, and He is the cause of universal transcendental bliss. He is always existing with brilliance.**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Today your dedicated servants bow down with straw between their teeth, gratitude vibrating from their hearts, and loving emotion emanating from their souls. Once again we have a great opportunity to please you with love and devotion, with simplicity, respect, and humble feelings as we attempt to express our appreciation, gratitude, and love for Your Divine Grace and for the priceless treasure of Kṛṣṇa consciousness you delivered to us here in New York City. New York City is your city, Śrīla Prabhupāda, the perfect field of unqualified, unfortunate, suffering conditioned souls who, though unqualified, are nonetheless attracted to the mystical activities of *bhakti-yoga,* and who also have respectful appreciation for those seeds of *kṛṣṇa-bhakti* you plant freely, thereby delivering the highest and only true important culture of mind, body, and self with the culture of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, propounded by Your Divine Grace on behalf of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu, the Golden Avatāra, Lord *Gaurāṅga*, the Supreme Dancer, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindadeva combined, Pañca-tattva in five features, an ocean of mercy—Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, Śrī *Nityānanda* Prabhu, Śrī Advaita Gosañi, Śrī Gadādhara Prabhu, and Śrī Śrīvāsa Prabhu in this infamous Naked Sinful Capital of doom and gloom, New York City, an ocean of conditioned, miserable, lost, and suffering *vibhinnāṁśa jīva-tattvas*.

In the entire history of this metropolitan center of sin and so-called culture, New York City, perhaps billions of persons have visited this mundane crossroads to see, taste, and relish the pain of this proclaimed unique culture capital and its unlimited tempestuous delights, which in reality are dry grains of sand, dust, and discarded straw plaguing the suffering conditioned hearts of all the millions and millions who come here to see that which does not have a permanent basis in truth compared to the ultimate reality of Godhead. People often like to live a lie and at the same time hear a sweet, alluring Manhattan Island serenade of illusion, which is unsatisfactory sustenance for the denizens of the hell-bound worlds. In contrast to all those unfortunate millions of *jīvātmās* who have dared enter the borders of New York City attempting to find happiness and pleasure yet did not know that only misery and pain blossomed on the trees in New York City, you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, Your Divine Grace, came to this unholy *dhāma* as the servant, friend, and well-wisher of all, to save and deliver us with the most potent medicine, the true cure, the real path leading to ultimate happiness and the highest bliss and eternal love realized by complete Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Therefore you are without doubt the greatest and most magnanimous person who ever entered New York City. Being infallible, Your Divine Grace is always transcendental to the modes of nature binding all the unfortunate suffering *jīvātmas,* yet out of humility and love you act as their servant, their ever well-wisher, and their true friend, and it is by such great qualities that we recognize Your Divine Grace as the great master of all, the master at whose feet all other masters sit: Śrīla *Prabhupāda*. So rare and beneficent is the appearance and association of such a great and magnanimous Vaiṣṇava *ācārya* as Your Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda that for a million births a *jīva* can wander this *mahat-tattva* not recognizing even one other entity as a true friend, yet when the pure devotee of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi gets off the boat and walks in the midst of those suffering pilgrims of doom and gloom, they recognize and understand instantly upon meeting such a rare Vaiṣṇava *ācārya,* a true servant of the servant of the servant, that this great and wonderful person, although I have never saw him before, never heard him speak before, yet somehow I deeply sense, understand, and know that he is my best and true friend, my ever well-wisher. Śrīla Prabhupāda, your pure potent Kṛṣṇa conscious *śakti* awakens the sleeping ears and eyes of the deaf and blind, your super potent transmission of the mercy of Śrī Kṛṣṇa enters the mind and heart of those fortunate enough to gain contact with Your Divine Grace, and your *kṛṣṇa-śakti bhāva* delivers revelation, realization, *divya*-*jñāna,* and real wisdom, and like a powerful dose of smelling salts your Kṛṣṇa consciousness *śakti* vibration brings the miserable out of the deep dark coma of fear, misery, and hell.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you deliver the gift of love for Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, originally emanating from the golden heart of Lord *Gaurāṅga* and then transmitted through the pure medium of great Vaiṣṇava *ācāryas,* through the blissful loving hearts of Śrī *Rūpa* *Gosvāmī* and Śrī Sanātana *Gosvāmī*, Śrī Svarūpa Dāmodara *Gosvāmī* and Raghunātha Dāsa *Gosvāmī*, as well as Śrīla Jīva *Gosvāmī*. That tide of love of Kṛṣṇa flows like an unstoppable spiritual tsunami through the hearts of the confidential associates of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Śrī *Nityānanda* *Rāma*. Those great Vaiṣṇava *ācāryas* who also render pure *prema-bhakti sevā* to the supreme fountainhead of love and bliss, the Parampāda Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu, at the apex, the superexcellent station of Kṛṣṇa and the spiritual place of transcendental pastimes of Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s *nitya-līlā rasas*.

Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindadeva, who are none other than the supreme ocean of mercy, Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu, who sends forth incessant nectar of devotion, *samudra* waves of *bhāva* and love, to give freely in this Age of Kali what no incarnation of Kṛṣṇa ever offered before, the eternal, highest taste, the sweetest *rasa,* Śrī *Rādhā*-Kṛṣṇa *prema-bhakti sevā*. Enabling even the unqualified ultimately, after revival of complete Kṛṣṇa consciousness, to gain entrance into the topmost abode of truth, that place from which, once one has attained it, one will never return. The highest paradise *dhāma* of Śrī Goloka Vṛndāvana, Kṛṣṇaloka.

No one can buy a ticket to that Goloka Vṛndāvana *dhāma,* Śrīla Prabhupāda. Such tickets are issued only by Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi when Your Divine Grace is pleased upon us for a lifetime of sincere loving *sevā,* when Your Divine Grace is pleased with the perfection of our loving service sentiment to please and love Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindadeva, and the wonderful Vaiṣṇava devotees, and when Your Divine Grace is pleased upon us for dedicating our life and as many future births as you allow us to serve as your instruments, distributing the salve of *kṛṣṇa-prema,* the salve of love, by giving the holy names of the Pañca-tattva *mahā-mantra* and the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*, and by giving billions of doses of the topmost medicine of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, your Bhaktivedanta Book Trust words of wisdom. The highest salvation, the *bṛhat-mṛdaṅga,* the real panacea of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, the cure the conditioned heart cries and hankers for, birth after birth.

The Vaikuṇṭha waves of *kṛṣṇa-prema* continue by the unstoppable flow of *kṛṣṇa-prema* through pure-hearted Vaiṣṇava *ācārya* saints such as Śrīla Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja *Gosvāmī* and Śrīla Narottama Dāsa Ṭhākura, then Śrīla Baladeva Vidyābhūṣaṇa and Śrīla Jagannātha Dāsa Bābājī Mahārāja, along with Śrīla Sac-cid-ānanda Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura and Śrīla Gaurakiśora Dāsa Bābājī Mahārāja, who deliver the great wave of *kṛṣṇa-prema* to the pure and magnanimous Kṛṣṇa conscious heart of the great Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, who transfers the flow of the Kṛṣṇaloka *samudra* wave of *prema* to Your Divine Grace, and your pure, empowered, sacred loving heart sends forth unlimited quantities of spiritual seeds of *kṛṣṇa-prema,* causing simultaneous inundation and tilling of the entire Western world hearts of *jīvātmās* suffering, miserable, and all-too-often living a life of lies. You start the process of reclamation and reformation of these unfortunate children of Kṛṣṇa by the all-powerful mercy of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi.

Those Kṛṣṇa-ized seeds of devotional action gradually begin the annihilation of the mundane way of activity of a lost civilization rapidly gliding toward hell. The roar of that wave of *kṛṣṇa-prema* is now louder than all mundane sound in the universe combined, yet mystically it delivers divine silence and peace to those who drown in that great Vaikuṇṭha high tide of *kṛṣṇa-prema,* to those fortunate souls whose only last cries when drowning in that tidal wave of bliss are inspired by love for Kṛṣṇa and can be heard in the harmonious sound divine of His Pañca-tattva *mahā-mantra*—*jaya śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya prabhu nityānanda śrī-advaita gadādhara śrīvāsādi-gaura-bhakta-vṛnda—*and in the Great Chant for Deliverance, the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*—Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

Yet that great Pañca-tattva tidal wave of *kṛṣṇa-prema* does not stop after drowning the entire Western world, but recedes with equally powerful force, equal mercy, equal peace and bliss, and equal love of Kṛṣṇa; the great mystic Vaikuṇṭha high tide of divine *kṛṣṇa-prema* recedes with the power of Lord *Gaurāṅga*’s, Lord Nityānanda’s, and the Pañca-tattva’s oceanic mercy, and with unprecedented loving results. Back to the source, the epicenter of distribution, the land of Bhārata, the great Bhārata Bhūmi, where the original Personality of Godhead, Bhagavān Śrī Kṛṣṇa, displays His divine eternal *līlā* of bliss and love. Back to India, back to Vṛndāvana *dhāma,* back to Śrīdhām Māyāpur, yes, back to Godhead, until the inundation of *kṛṣṇa-prema* is complete, having covered all worldly regions with a love strong enough to deliver all living entities, who joyfully drown in the loud roar of that great unstoppable Pañca-tattva wave of eternal peace, knowledge, bliss, and love. Finally all spirit souls blessed by this great International Society for Krishna Consciousness can together, all at once, be heard crying out one last time in total desperation yet with unflinching faith and unbounded joy, “Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare,” as the tidal wave of *kṛṣṇa-prema* finally ends forever the temporary days and nights of misery for all the great souls so fortunate and blessed to drown in the ocean of the nectar of bliss, love, and pure devotion in the great *kṛṣṇa-prema* wave sent forth by the causeless mercy of Lord *Gaurāṅga* and Lord *Nityānanda*.

*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto 'pi*  
*dhyāyan stuvaṁs tasya yaśas tri-sandhyaṁ*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

**“By the mercy of the spiritual master one receives the benediction of Kṛṣṇa. Without the grace of the spiritual master, one cannot make any advancement. Therefore, I should always remember and praise the spiritual master. At least three times a day I should offer my respectful obeisance’s unto the lotus feet of my spiritual master.”**

Please be kind to all of us today, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please bless us with complete Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Please bless us with causeless devotion at your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda, birth after birth. Please bless us with causeless devotion at the lotus feet of the servants of the servants of the servants of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the maintainer of the *gopīs.* And please bless us with causeless devotion at the lotus feet of the servants of the servants of the servants of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Śrī *Nityānanda* *Rāma*, who plundered the storehouse of love for Śrī Kṛṣṇa and drowned the whole world in a great wave of love of God.

Your unfortunate, insignificant beggars at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindadeva mandira, Brooklyn, New York City, New York, USA.  
(written by Rāmabhadra Dāsa)

## Noida

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble and respectful obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet! All glories to your Vyāsa-pūjā!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, this is the most important day of our lives, for this divine day offers us an opportunity to look back to the time when you mercifully came into our lives. Our lives are divided into two parts: before Śrīla Prabhupāda and after Śrīla Prabhupāda, that is to say, before you imparted transcendental knowledge to us through your magical purports, and after you did so.

*Śrīla Prabhupāda*, although we took birth in India, the land of dharma, before we met you we did not know who God is, who we are, or what the significance of the human form of life is. Lord Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, imparted His instructions to *Brahmā*, Vyāsadeva complied them in the form of scriptures, and Gaṇeśa assisted by writing them down, but we were so dumb that we didn’t understood the Lord’s statements or put much value on them—until you showered your mercy on all of us without discrimination by giving us crystal-clear purports to the verses of the scriptures.

Not only had did you present *Bhagavad-gītā* “as it is,” but through your masterful commentaries you also presented the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* *Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Īśopaniṣad,* and other works as they are. Śrīla Prabhupāda, only through your books can we understand the real meaning of our lives. You empowered us with the knowledge of the self and the Absolute and prepared us for the war against sense gratification. You equipped us so well that just by reading, relishing, and repeating the instructions in your books we can survive today, when the *Māyā* is attacking from all directions all the time.

Although, to be honest, we are not following your instructions 100%, amazingly we are still able to fight the war against materialism, the war against consumerism, the war against our lusty environment, the war against impersonalism, the war against ritvikism, the war against the economic recession, and so on. We can just imagine the status of sincere devotees who take your words as their life and soul 100%. They are surely going back to Godhead, to the planet where you are waiting to continue engaging them in the loving devotional service of Kṛṣṇa. This is the inconceivable potency of your books: merely by reading them one becomes qualified to read more, and eventually one is able to hear and chant the holy name of the Lord purely.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please be merciful to us and teach us how to follow Your Divine Grace’s instructions 100%. We are sure our life will be blissful and meaningful by cooperatively working to take ISKCON to the next level, but that will be possible only if we can imbibe your mood without any misinterpretation.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, under your ISKCON umbrella your project at Noida is progressing toward completion by *Nityānanda* Trayodaśī 2014. But for this to actually happen we need your blessings, inspiration, and guidance more than ever. *Śrīla Prabhupāda*, depending upon Kṛṣṇa and your spiritual master, His Divine Grace Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura Prabhupāda, you created the greatest miracle in the three worlds by transforming countless sinners into saints. Your Divine Grace traveled tirelessly at an advanced age, when any other devotee would have preferred to sit and do his *bhajana* in one place. But you exemplified compassion by reaching into the heart of millions of conditioned souls and personally awakening their love for Kṛṣṇa. You taught us everything—from cooking *roṭī* to wearing *dhotī,* from dressing to addressing, from courtesy to mercy, and much more. Your Divine Grace established many temples, where people can come and find out the root cause of all their miseries and leave with spiritual solutions.

We devotees at Noida are struggling to finish one temple, and this brief statement shows our incompetence, which is a result of not following your instructions 100%. Śrīla Prabhupāda, since we have a strong desire to place another jewel in your brilliant crown by inaugurating the Noida project in February, 2014, please purify us so that we can cooperatively work hard and complete the project on schedule for the pleasure of you, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindadeva, and all the Vaiṣṇavas.

On this special day we are unable to provide you with a worthy gift, but just like dependent children we beg you for a gift in the form of love for your books. Please bestow this benediction upon us. We are very sure that just by this benediction we will be able to successfully perform our services in ISKCON and carry on the legacy of Lord Caitanya with rekindled vigor and sincerity.

Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s Vyāsa-pūjā mahotsava kī jaya!

Your insignificant servants at ISKCON’s Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindadeva mandir and the Bhaktivedanta Academy for Spiritual Science, in Noida, India.

## Ouaga

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

**We offer our respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

In the Sixth Canto of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* the great sage Dadhīci offers his body to the demigods so that from his bones they can make a thunderbolt to kill Vṛtrāsura, but before doing so he makes this all-important observation;

*yo ’dhruveṇātmanā nāthā na dharmaṁ na yaśaḥ pumān*  
*īheta bhūta-dayayā sa śocyaḥ sthāvarair api*

**“O demigods, one who has no compassion for humanity in its suffering and does not sacrifice his impermanent body for the higher causes of religious principles or eternal glory is certainly pitied even by the immovable beings.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **6.10.8)**

In the purport to this verse you write wonderfully about Lord Caitanya and the Six Gosvāmīs:

*tyaktvā sudustyaja-surepsita-rājya-lakṣmīṁ*  
*dharmiṣṭha ārya-vacasā yad agād araṇyam*  
*māyā-mṛgaṁ dayitayepsitam anvadhāvad*  
*vande mahā-puruṣa te caraṇāravindam*

**“We offer our respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of the Lord, upon whom one should always meditate. He left His householder life, leaving aside His eternal consort, whom even the denizens of heaven adore. He went into the forest to deliver the fallen souls, who are put into illusion by material energy.”**

To accept *sannyāsa* means to commit civil suicide, but *sannyāsa* is compulsory, at least for every *brāhmaṇa*, every first-class human being. Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu had a very young and beautiful wife and a very affectionate mother. Indeed, the affectionate dealings of His family members were so pleasing that even the demigods could not expect such happiness at home. Nevertheless, for the deliverance of all the fallen souls of the world, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu took *sannyāsa* and left home when He was only twenty-four years old. He lived a very strict life as a *sannyāsī,* refusing all bodily comforts. Similarly, His disciples the Six Gosvāmīs were ministers who held exalted positions in society, but they also left everything to join the movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Śrīnivāsa Ācārya says:

*tyaktvā tūrṇam aśeṣa-maṇḍala-pati-śreṇīṁ sadā tucchavat*  
*bhūtvā dīna-gaṇeśakau karuṇayā kaupīna-kanthāśritau*

These Gosvāmīs left their very comfortable lives as ministers, Zamindars, and learned scholars and joined Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s movement just to show mercy to the fallen souls of the world (*dīna-gaṇeśakau karuṇayā*). Accepting very humble lives as mendicants, wearing no more than loincloths and torn quilts (*kaupīna-kantha*), they lived in Vṛndāvana and followed Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s order to excavate Vṛndāvana’s lost glories.

Following in the footsteps of the Gosvāmīs, you also left your family and business and went to Vṛndāvana, where you took up residence at the Rādhā-Dāmodara temple and prepared for your great preaching mission. And even when ISKCON became established and had many centers around the world, you sacrificed every bit of your vital energy to translate the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* giving your Bhaktivedanta purports for the benefit of the entire world. What a great sacrifice you made, giving your life for the benefit of humanity! Even on your disappearance bed you were absorbed in giving the world your deep realizations of the message of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* speaking till the very end. For several months you stopped catering to the needs of your body; what mattered to you was delivering *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* to the world. Who could be more compassionate to the people of this world than you, Śrīla Prabhupāda?

Dadhīci Muni gave his body so one demon, Vṛtrāsura, could be killed, but you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, sacrificed your life to kill the demonic mentalities of millions of conditioned souls, thus turning them into *kṛṣṇa-bhaktas*. The evidence of your success is everywhere: throughout the world there are temples of Kṛṣṇa, the holy names of Kṛṣṇa are being chanted on the streets of the cities around the world, and Govinda’s restaurants are opening up everywhere, giving many people the opportunity to accept *kṛṣṇa-prasādam* and give up the cruelty of meat-eating. Who can be more compassionate to the people of this world than you, Śrīla Prabhupāda? All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Your servants in Ouaga, Burkina Faso, West Africa.  
(written by Śrīvāsa Dāsa)

## Pandharpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respectful obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace! All glories to your appearance day!

The more the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement expands, the more we realize your greatness. By your mercy and the inspired direction of His Holiness Lokanāth Swami, ISKCON Pandharpur is flourishing. Our youth preaching and congregational preaching are increasing, the standards of the *brahmacārī* *āśrama* are being maintained, every resident is regularly attending the morning program, and we are celebrating festivals on a grand scale.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, this year at Śrīdhāma Pandharpur, the abode of Lord Kṛṣṇa and Rukmiṇī Devī, we started a wonderful project in memory of you. We are constructing a big *ghāṭa* in your name. It will be known as Śrīla Prabhupāda Ghāṭa. This *ghāṭa* will have special features like *dīpamālās,* *toranas,* a space for *bhajana* and *kīrtana,* a stage, pavilions, and utility rooms. There will also be a beautiful garden, with trees from Vṛndāvana and other nice greenery, along with a diorama exhibition depicting the Lord’s pastimes.

ISKCON is well known in India for building temples, but this is the first time a *ghāṭa* will spread your glory and fame all over, and that also in Śrīdhāma Pandharpur. We assure you this *ghāṭa* will be very beautiful and become a landmark in Pandharpur. It will be like an open temple that will preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness to whoever comes to take a bath in the holy river Chandrabhaga in order to get her blessings and be freed from all sinful reactions.

The Śrīla Prabhupāda Ghāṭa is a unanimously approved project of the IRGB and the Indian WIDC committee. We have a board of directors for this project composed of all our stalwart devotees, headed by His Holiness Lokanāth Swami. Under their guidance and with your blessings we’ll be able to meet the challenges and see this project through to a successful conclusion. Lord Caitanya visited Pandharpur in 1514 A.D., so we are planning to hold the opening ceremony of this auspicious project next year, commemorating the 500th anniversary of Lord Caitanya’s visit.

This project is expected to cost more than two million dollars, and all the devotees of Pandharpur are trying their best to fulfill this dream as early as possible. This ghāṭa will facilitate millions of pilgrims who visit Pandharpur for *darśana* of Lord Viṭhṭhala and take a bath in the holy river Chandrabhaga.

The Śrīla Prabhupāda Ghāṭa will be a divine offering at your lotus feet, symbolizing our deep gratitude and love for you. Please shower your blessings on us so we can complete this beautiful project successfully.

We pray to be engaged in your service eternally.  
Your servants at ISKCON Pandharpur, India.

## Paris

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We offer our most humble obeisances to the dust of your divine lotus feet, to your ISKCON, and to all your wonderful disciples. Unlimited glories to Your Divine Grace, who descended from the spiritual abode to deliver the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu!

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please kindly accept this little offering for your Vyāsa-pūjā as a token of our gratefulness. We remember you every day with love and gratitude. Your Vyāsa-pūjā gives us a special opportunity to try to describe in words what is inexpressible.

So, today again we are in your sweet, sweet Vṛndāvana. As you said, “Bombay is my office, Mayapur is my place of worship, but Vṛndāvana is my home.” Home sweet home.

As the *senapati* of Śrī Caitanya Mahaprabhu, and on the instruction of your Guru Mahārāja, you left this paradise in your late sixties and went to the hell of New York City in the middle of hippie time to transform it into a happy time. At great hardship you traveled on the cargo ship *Jaladuta* to bring us your Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa *prema-bhakti*, eventually establishing 108 temples all around the world and giving us the *mahā-mantra*.

You also visited our country, France, and our “city of love,” Paris, to install Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Parīśvara on rue Lesueur. “Śrīmatī *Rādhārāṇī* is the most beautiful of the Parisian girls,” you said, “with her Parisīśvara, the master of Paris.” It was on August 10, 1973. Thank you so much, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Now, forty years later, after a long stay outside of Paris (Ermenonville, Noisy-le-Grand), your Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Parisīśvara are staying in Sarcelles, a suburb north of Paris, where They at least own the property, and where we are holding daily programs, Sunday Feasts, and regular festivals.

Our dear lord and master, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are fully dependant on your causeless mercy to continue rendering service to Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Parisīśvara. When we were asked by our temple president, Nitāi Gaurasundara Dāsa, to write this humble homage for your Vyāsa-pūjā, for inspiration we read some old Vyāsa-pūjā books, and in the book published just twenty years ago, commemorating your appearance anniversary on 12 August 1993, your devotees in Paris wrote, “We humbly beg you to give us the force to be able to re-install Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Parisīśvara in Paris itself, as you did exactly twenty years ago.” At the installation you instructed your followers to distribute your books and continue to bring in new devotees so that this movement will always continue to expand.

Also, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your pleasure we have recently been able to reprint here in Vṛndāvana five hundred copies of a French booklet called “Vivre Vīgītarien,” written by your disciples in Switzerland. We hope that by your mercy this booklet will encourage many people to become vegetarian and experience the higher taste of *kṛṣṇa-prasādam.* We also pray that Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Parisīśvara may soon be able to return to the city of love, Paris, to again attract hundreds and thousands of people who are still looking for the opportunity to learn the goal of human life: to chant and be happy!

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for everything you have given us. You are truly the well-wisher of all and everyone. Please inspire us to distribute your gifts to others. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for appearing in this world as our eternal well-wisher and savior. We will forever remain most grateful to Your Divine Grace.

Jagat Guru Śrīla Prabhupāda kī jaya!

The devotees of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Parisīśvara at Their temple near Paris, France.  
(written by Ojasvī Dāsa and Gītāmṛta Devī Dāsī)

## Perth

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to your divine lotus feet!

On this auspicious day of your appearance, we, your aspiring servants in Perth, would like to thank you for all that you have done for us, and to the best of our ability we wish to glorify your innumerable wonderful qualities. Most of us here are your granddisciples, and therefore we never had the opportunity to experience your physical association. Despite this, however, we feel so blessed for all the mercy you are constantly bestowing upon us.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, as Lord Caitanya’s most intimate associate, you have given us everything that is truly of value in this world. Not content simply to remain in Vṛndāvana during your old age, you underwent many austerities so that people like us, all around the world, would be able to learn about your beloved Lord Kṛṣṇa and the wonderful process of blissfully serving Him with devotion. You personally brought to the Western world the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra,* which is nondifferent from the Lord Himself, and the books you translated so painstakingly are the guidebooks by which we can happily live our lives and ultimately return home, back to Godhead.

If it weren’t for your compassion, where would we be? As your aspiring followers, we owe you so much. Not only were you the perfect teacher, but you were also the ideal disciple of your own spiritual master, Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī Ṭhākura. Taking his instructions as your life and soul, you always meditated on how to become “a very good English preacher” so you could share this knowledge with lost souls all around the world. In this way you fulfilled the desire of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and the previous *ācāryas* that the holy names would be chanted in every town and village. Although at times the obstacles appeared insurmountable, you had not the slightest doubt that this would come to pass. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, although we can never repay you, we pray that we may always meditate on your impeccable example and follow in your footsteps by engaging wholeheartedly in the *saṅkīrtana* movement, taking it far and wide here in western Australia.

Perth is often described as one of the most remote cities in the world. Situated on the shore of the Indian Ocean, on the east it is separated from the nearest large cities by thousands of kilometers of desert and near-desert. But the mercy of Lord Caitanya and you, His dear servant, knows no boundaries, and so despite this city’s isolation, over the years ISKCON Perth has evolved from a tiny preaching center into a sizable devotee community that continues to grow vigorously.

We pray that the growth in our Perth *yātrā* in recent years has pleased you, *Śrīla Prabhupāda*. It’s now about five years since our temple moved from its former location in Bayswater to larger premises in Kalamunda, and it’s astounding to see how many new, sincere people have been inspired to regularly visit the temple and seriously take up the process of devotional service. Many of these new devotees are taking on responsibilities within the community—organizing your Sunday Feast programs, holding congregational gatherings, participating in *harināma* *saṅkīrtana* parties, and holding classes where your books are studied. Their enthusiasm is contagious, and it’s inspiring for the older devotees to see how your example and instructions continue to transform people’s lives.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you so much for giving us this most rare and wonderful gift of devotional service. On this holy day we pray that you will continue to shower your mercy upon every one of us and on our small, isolated *yātrā*. Please bestow upon us the spiritual strength to fully take shelter of the Lord’s holy names, the intelligence to understand your books, the sincerity to follow your instructions, and the enthusiasm to wholeheartedly immerse ourselves in Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement.

Your aspiring servants at ISKCON Perth, Australia.

## Philadelphia

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you on the auspicious occasion of your appearance!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, our spiritual community—full of festivals, classes, daily Deity worship, feasts, and friendship—owes its existence to your dedication, vision, and compassion. Because of you, on Sundays we have a place to join our friends, family, and guests in dancing, chanting, and feasting in service to Lord Jagannātha. Because of you, we jubilantly join together on Janmāṣṭamī night, crowding in front of the altar doors while calling out the holy name in eager anticipation of Rādhā-Śaradbihārīs midnight *darśana*. Because of you, we spend days, weeks, and months planning and preparing every last detail of Lord Jagannātha’s Ratha-yātrā, when He majestically parades down the streets of Philadelphia, where thousands of people see His face and hear the holy name for the first time. Because of you, we have Saturday night home programs, enlivening gatherings, *prasādam* at Govinda’s restaurant, and classes that inspire and deepen our understanding of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Every opportunity we have to engage in devotional service together—to participate in *kīrtana,* to circumambulate a massive hill made of sweets in honor of Govardhana Pūjā, to pack far more people into a kitchen than should humanly be possible out of enthusiasm to cook a masterpiece meal for the Lord—all of these opportunities exist because of you, because of your dedication to fulfill the desires of your dear spiritual master and your compassion to engage each and every one of us in pure devotional service. Your mercy has not only transformed the lives of individuals, but it has formed lasting devotional communities where fellow congregants have not only become our friends but have become our spiritual family.

In Philadelphia, attendance at our weekly Sunday Feast program and festivals continues to increase. Our Sunday School program for children is also growing and is led by highly qualified and dedicated teachers. To help better care for our children, this year we’ve significantly expanded our playground and have revitalized our Child Protection Team. Our devotional activities extend beyond the temple walls as well—from home programs throughout the region and regular Bhakti Vriksha gatherings in Bensalem and Delaware, to classes and the First Friday *kīrtana* at our downtown Govinda’s restaurant, to our ever-expanding and enlivening book distribution activities organized by Team Rādhā-Śaradbihārī, who have so far broken every goal they set for distributing your books. Ratha-yātrā also continues to be one of the foremost events in our community, engaging everyone in the massive task of pulling Lord Jagannātha down the city streets in procession along with Śrī Pañca-tattva. Ratha-yātrā also includes multiple *kīrtana* parties and even Scottish bagpipers piping tunes accompanying the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*.

Along with our accomplishments, however, also comes the challenge of working together and building relationships. We don’t always see eye to eye, and we don’t always steer clear of Vaiṣṇava *aparādha*. We owe it to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to make a genuine effort to work on and through these issues—to honestly communicate with one another, to develop a mood of humble service, and to consciously cultivate relationships where hearing, glorifying, and serving Kṛṣṇa are in the center.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us the priceless gift of Kṛṣṇa’s holy names and the opportunity for devotional service. Moreover, you have given us communities in which we can gain inspiration, guidance, friendship, and support in our devotional endeavors.

On this auspicious occasion, therefore, we thank you for the invaluable opportunity you have given us to develop and partake in a spiritual community. And we beg for your guidance as we endeavor to increase our own Kṛṣṇa consciousness as individuals as well as a community, and make a sincere effort to give Kṛṣṇa consciousness to others in the mood of serving Lord Caitanya’s mission.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we beg to eternally remain in your humble service.  
Your servants at ISKCON of Philadelphia, Pennsylvania, USA.

## Phoenix (Mauritius)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

By keeping you in the center each day, we can feel your causeless mercy flowing unconditionally in our execution of devotional service.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us such wonderful services that we can engage in for the pleasure of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Golokānanda. We can never begin to repay you. As a humble attempt to begin to fulfill your prediction that Mauritius would become the first Kṛṣṇa conscious nation, we plan to build a glorious temple for Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Golokānanda at Hare Krishna Land in Mauritius. Visitors will be able to have *darśana* of Their Lordships and buy your books in the gift shop. Other attractions will include a restaurant, theater, and *gurukula*. We are working with architects and designers now to finalize the plans.

We wish to showcase everything that Kṛṣṇa consciousness has to offer in the most attractive and beneficial way by sharing the precious, fundamental, and universal values of the treasure of divine love and everlasting joy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, our fervent desire is to please you. Please kindly guide us in all that we do. We shall never forget the magnitude of the good fortune you bestowed upon us.

Only at moments of deep reflection do we begin to truly appreciate what you have done for us and all humanity. We pray that these moments of awakening will be transformed into constant remembrance of your gifts with deep gratitude.

Your humble servants in Phoenix, Mauritius.

## Phoenix

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

With all love and respect we bow to you myriad times and pray that you please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you and to the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu! *Jaya* Nimāi! *Jaya* Nitāi!

Please guide us in our quest for sincere and unmotivated devotional service. It is by your mercy that we chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, calling out to Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, and that we have come to know Lord Caitanya, the incarnation of the Divine Couple. You introduced us to the Ādi Guru, Lord Balarāma, in the form of Lord *Nityānanda*. By His mercy the most wonderful and Blissful *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu is delivering all the Jagāis and Mādhāis of this world. Your Humility, patience, firm conviction, knowledge, compassion for the fallen souls, and Unconditional love for Kṛṣṇa and faith in the holy name and your Guru Mahārāja have induced Śrī Pañca-tattva to shower all blessings upon you and your mission in the service of Kṛṣṇa. You are Always ready to guide everyone back home, back to Godhead. You continue to inspire millions in Devotional service, and your instructions are forever present in your books. May we Always remember Kṛṣṇa and never forget Him.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, at times the path of devotional service is obscured by the darkness of our ignorance, false ego, and lack of humility, respect, and tolerance. We beg you to please forgive our mistakes and be the light on our path, our motivation to strengthen our character, and the captain of our ship as we navigate the contaminated waters of the Age of Kali. Please lead us in our quest for Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Please bless us to become pure devotees and, like you, preach and lead by example.

Your servants at ISKCON of Phoenix, Arizona, USA.

## Phuket

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you and your International Society for Krishna Consciousness!

We are very fortunate to have the opportunity to serve Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa in this den of *māyā* known as Phuket. People come here from all corners of the earth to try to enjoy endless varieties of gross sense gratification, which proliferate in this place. We also came here for sense gratification, but we found the ultimate sense gratification: gratifying Kṛṣṇa’s senses in the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We are very fortunate to have been tricked by Lord Kṛṣṇa. We thought we would enjoy some material pleasures here but have instead been given the greatest spiritual pleasure: chanting Kṛṣṇa’s holy names. Please allow us to go on chanting and to experience the true nectar of the name.

With deepest gratitude,  
Your devotees at ISKCON Phuket, Thailand

## Richmond Hill

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*  
*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Once again, it is the most auspicious day of your appearance,  
A time to give thanks and honor your perseverance,  
Yet there are not enough words that would suffice or provide coherence.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you gave us a new life,  
A reprieve from material entanglement and strife,  
A home in which bhakti-yoga was rife.  
Fulfilling the mission of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu,  
You imparted His message to those that had no clue;  
You proved again and again that you are the bona fide guru.  
You provided answers and solutions in all areas;  
To be devoted and sincere were the only criteria;  
By chanting and dancing we could be in euphoria.  
You traveled the globe fourteen times,  
Giving out the message of Godhead so sublime,  
Showing us the path to not come back time after time.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are truly a miracle!  
What you accomplished is inconceivable;  
This was predicted—you are that individual.  
With so many books of knowledge,  
Each and every page translated in language after language—  
For this the universe must pay you homage.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, because of your tapasya  
You pulled us karmīs from the clutches of māyā  
And made us understand the laws of karma.  
Happiness in this material world is elusive,  
No matter how one’s pursuits may be aggressive;  
You showed us that only Kṛṣṇa consciousness is effective.  
We must read and follow your vāṇī;  
Everything we need to know is there, you guarantee.  
We must surrender and nurture the seed of bhakti.  
You gave us so much, from the Brahma-saṁhitā  
To the vibrations of the Hare Kṛṣṇa mahā-mantra;  
For these you are certainly ācārya.  
You taught all of us everything so lovingly,  
From cooking to painting to serving the Deity;  
We must continue to follow with sincerity.  
You blessed us with the writings of Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī,  
Imparted the importance of fasts like Ekādaśī,  
And gave us the joy of festival after festival like Janmāṣṭamī.  
You wanted book distribution to be the priority,  
Knowing that your writings could deliver humanity;  
To follow these instructions is our duty.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are Lord Kṛṣṇa’s representative;  
You effectively dispelled all that was speculative;  
All your instructions are based on śāstra and decisive.  
By the saṅkīrtana movement the world is now saturated:  
Practically every country ISKCON has cultivated,  
And this growth continues and will continue unabated.  
Your ISKCON movement continues to flourish  
As saṅkīrtana hundreds of thousands of devotees get to relish;  
Being in the service of Lord Kṛṣṇa is their only wish.  
You gave us Kṛṣṇa’s divine message, emphasizing “Books are the basis,”  
Through the likes of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and *Bhagavad-gītā* *As It Is*.  
What more could we ask for—it is simply bliss!  
You taught us all step by step, by example;  
The way you managed us neophytes was incredible;  
Everything you did was purely impeccable.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, we can never adequately thank you for this treasure;  
All that you have given us is beyond measure;  
We wish to serve you and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa forever.  
Please always keep us at your lotus feet;  
Your glories we wish to repeat and repeat;  
Only then can our lives be complete.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda! Jaya Gurudeva!

Your humble servants in Richmond Hill, Canada.  
(written by Mīnākṣī Devī Dāsī)

## Rome

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances unto your lotus feet. All glories to you, to your beloved spiritual master, and to the previous *ācāryas*, whom you represent and whose mission you’ve taken up despite the powerful headwinds of Kali-yuga.

When in the course of your preaching work you came to Rome for the first time, the Pope would not receive you, but that didn’t bother you at all, and you humbly requested your disciples to establish ISKCON centers in Rome for the sake of the fallen. Since then, there have always been Hare Kṛṣṇa devotees, temples, and restaurants in what is known as The Eternal City. Now it is our turn to serve you in Rome, to take advantage of the opportunity to taste the nectar of your mercy.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, where would we be now if on that day in Saint Peter’s Square you had not given your eternal blessings to our service! We were not even born yet! What could we now say to others if we didn’t have your books, in which you are eternally present? How would our lives have ended up without the shelter of your lotus feet? And how many more lifetimes would we have wasted far away from the safe and holy land of Kṛṣṇa consciousness?

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, always stay with us. Always keep us with you. Shower your compassion upon us so that we can serve you. Empower us in our service unto you. We are confident that you will not refuse to show us your mercy, since we are begging for it by the grace of our spiritual masters, who are themselves manifestations of your mercy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda kī jaya!

Your aspiring servants in Rome, Italy.  
(written by Harikīrtana Dāsa)

## St. Louis

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

We don’t have any patience, it’s true.  
How can we expect to be anything but blue?  
A poet said (I forgot his name)  
that we have no one but ourselves to blame.  
Prabhupāda, we all here are grateful that you we’ve met;  
Your message, clear and bold, says “There’s hope as yet!”  
You didn’t say it would be easy—  
There’d be strife and toil;  
It’s painful to lance a boil.  
Only by a standard you kept so pure  
are we able to feel that we can be sure  
that one day or other we’ll put things aside  
that bind us up and cause false pride.  
While we still can we want to say from our hearts:  
Thank you! Thank you for all, especially the Ratha-yātrā carts!

Your servants in St. Louis, Missouri, USA.

## Saltillo

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances. All glories to you, O glorious founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON!

In our attempt to serve your mission, we have committed many mistakes. Please forgive us for any offenses we have committed to your holy disciples and followers. Please help us optimize our preaching so that the leaders and educated class of this country, Mexico, can see us as representing something respectable and can appreciate the greatness of your books and the Vedic culture you taught worldwide.

We pray to you to please let us always be sheltered in the shade of your lotus feet. Please help us preach effectively so that every day more souls may come to surrender their lives at the lotus feet of *Śrī* *Śrī* Rādhā-Govinda in your temple here in Saltillo, Mexico.

Your servants in Saltillo, Mexico.

## San Antonio

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Although we are unqualified in many ways, we have been given the privilege and honor of writing our Vyāsa-pūjā offering to Your Divine Grace. Therefore, we will try our best to explain our deepest and most sincere appreciation for you and all you have shared with us.

Simply put, you have given the world hope and a spiritual shelter during harsh historical times, when co-existence is becoming more and more difficult. We pray that we can continue to help spread the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to as many people as possible in this city. This will be possible only because we have the loving relationships and structure you have given us. Please continue to guide us, and please forgive us for any shortcomings or offenses. Your are truly our father and grandfather and, above all, a flawless gift from God. Please allow us to continue to serve and take direction from your dear representative His Holiness Guru Prasāda Swami.

Our major focus this year has shifted to college programs. We have one college club at the local campus of the University of Texas and another at St. Mary’s University. We are starting to gain solid momentum and steadiness in our distribution of *prasādam* and books and in our weekly discourses on the transcendental literature you have provided us. Many of the students and staff are becoming strongly favorable toward our movement. We have also continued our community outreach activities through various festivals, concerts, and catering opportunities, cultivating many wonderful friendships along the way. Most recently, the mayor himself received a *Bhagavad-gītā*. Every day we pray for more of your causeless mercy.

We have been intensely engaged in your loving service and are now on the verge of owning our own property. Led by His Holiness Guru Prasāda Swami and His Grace Advaita Ācārya Prabhu, we pray to innovatively expand our project here by gaining more manpower at the temple. At times it can be very difficult to work with one another, for we come from various backgrounds. Still, we have overcome many obstacles together as a congregation and are maintaining our relationships quite well. It often seems as the though the things that separate us materially often draw us closer together spiritually. All that is required is faith and patience. Could you please ask Kṛṣṇa to instill those two requirements into hearts so we may not forget the reason you came here?

Your servants in San Antonio, Texas.  
(written by Bhagavān Nārada Dāsa)

## Seattle

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. We are forever indebted to you for providing us with ISKCON, where we can take shelter of Śrī Śrī Rādhā–Nīla-Mādhava by following your bona fide teachings.

There are millions of unfortunate souls in this world who have yet to see the *Bhagavad-gītā*. Thousands of people coming to the West from India have not seen or read the *Bhagavad-gītā*. One such example in our community was a south Indian Shaivite priest. Although born in a *brāhmaṇa* family, he lived for twenty-seven years without getting a chance to see the *Bhagavad-gītā.* Then he visited your Seattle temple, and by your mercy flowing through the devotees here, he came in touch with your *Bhagavad-gītā* *As It Is*. Later he and his family became Kṛṣṇa conscious under your care, shelter, and guidance. Now his family has moved back to India to further spread your movement in remote villages in India. This example proves that the knowledge imparted by you in disciplic succession is transcendental and transforms people naturally.

Your arrival in the USA preceded the arrival of masses of highly educated Indians over the last four or five decades. We see this wave of immigration as a grand plan of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s to connect these searching souls with Kṛṣṇa consciousness through the ISKCON temples in the West and your books. Your disciples are training these granddisciples as you envisioned. We have been led by one such disciple, Śrīman Harivilāsa Prabhu, who is furthering your mission in greater Seattle area with various innovative preaching methodologies and cultural presentations.

In your purport to *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.5.16 you write:

The expert devotees also can discover novel ways and means to convert the nondevotees in terms of particular time and circumstance. Devotional service is dynamic activity, and the expert devotees can find out competent means to inject it into the dull brains of the materialistic population.

By following this instruction, we can spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness in any part of the world. This is the motto we are trying to follow in Seattle. We are regularly reviewing and improving many of our outreach programs. Just to attract everyone to come and take *darśana* of Śrī Śrī Rādhā–Nīla-Mādhava, we have embellished the temple hall with elaborately carved woodwork. Our temple hall is going to attract thousands of people from all over. We are also managing temple resources wisely. To increase our eligibility for grants, we have engaged a reputable accounting firm to audit our finances. The Vedic school has been going strong for more than ten years and is training approximately 150 children. Two Bhakti Shastri courses are being conducted for children age 10-16. The Vedic University provides the same opportunity for adults. About 150 parents are attending these courses based on your teachings. About 60 adults are systematically studying your *Bhagavad-gītā* *As It Is*.

In your purport to *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.5.11 you write:

There is a great need for disseminating the message of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* all over the world. It is the duty of every responsible Indian to broadcast the transcendental message of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* throughout the world to do all the supermost good as well as to bring about the desired peace in the world.

We are trying to follow these sublime instructions. Taking this instruction into the core of our hearts, we are conducting various preaching, educational, and cultural presentations for the masses of people in Seattle.

For the first time in North America, in 2012 the BBT achieved a 20% year-to-year increase in remittances. To achieve this goal, every temple pitched in and distributed as many books as possible. At the Seattle temple, we achieved the same increase, distributing about fifty *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* sets in the process.

We pray at your lotus feet that you may continue to shower your unlimited mercy in this dark part of the world, where the sun shines fewer than 150 days a year. Your guidance is our shining sun to navigate the ocean of nescience called this material world. Without your loving hand over our heads, providing reassurance, we would not be able to do anything. We pray that you remain in our lives and that we continue to imbibe your teachings. We seek your blessings to continue to work together in furthering Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Your servants at ISKCON of Seattle, Washington, USA.

## Simhachalam

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you, who appeared as Jagad Guru to perfectly fulfill the mission of your Guru Mahārāja, Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī *Gosvāmī* Prabhupāda.

You are the supreme servitor of Kṛṣṇa. By our serving you and Lord Nṛsiṁhadeva, our lives have become meaningful.

You are the captain of the good ship ISKCON, which is crossing over the dangerous, fearful, formidable ocean of the Age of Kali, which deteriorates all the good qualities of human beings.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you underwent severe austerities to teach the world the path of devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa and Lord Nṛsiṁhadeva. You raised planet earth from its degradation and traveled at will all over the globe, spreading the glories of the Lord’s holy name.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, in the early 60s you were living alone in Vṛndāvana, at the Śrī *Śrī* Rādhā-Dāmodara temple, but you had a vision of establishing Hare Kṛṣṇa temples throughout the world. That vision came to pass, and one of those temples is in the Bavarian Forest, in Germany. It’s the temple of your beloved lords Śrī Śrī Prahlāda–Lakṣmī-Nṛsiṁhadevajī. You reside here in your Simhachalam temple and bestow your mercy upon the Simhachalam-vāsīs. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your mercy we have a community that is very sincerely serving you and Lord Nṛsiṁhadeva. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, permit us to live in the shade of your lotus feet so that we can continue widely distributing your nectarean books, *kṛṣṇa*-*prasādam,* and transcendental sound vibrations in the form of the holy names.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you so much for mercifully giving us Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

We beg to eternally remain servants at your lotus feet.

Your servants at Simhachalam, ISKCON’s temple in Bavaria, Germany.  
(written by Narahari Devī Dāsī)

## Stockholm

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respectful obeisances. All glories to you, the founder-*ācārya* of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness.

When Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, after a great endeavor to build the Bhag Bazaar temple in Kolkata, finally went in procession to take the Deities to the temple for installation, he sang a special song. In one of the verses he expressed a very significant realization and message to all followers of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu: “When Lord *Gaurāṅga* appeared, the *sādhus* became involved with the sense objects.”

As far as possible, traditional *sādhus* stay aloof from sense objects and material entanglement. They often withdraw from civilization and live like beggars, practicing spiritual disciplines in holy places. Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī Ṭhākura also lived like that for several years in Māyāpur, completing a vow to chant a billion holy names. But then he prepared to do something that looked completely different, namely, to start the preaching mission by moving to the materialistic city of Calcutta—a move that even seemed to go against the wishes of his spiritual master, Śrīla Gaura Kiśora Dāsa Bābājī, who said that he himself would never go to that “illusory universe, Calcutta.”

But he never actually went to Calcutta, just as you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, never actually left Vṛndāvana to go to the sinful Western world. On the contrary, as a *sādhu* in the line of Lord Caitanya, you instead took the spiritual world to the West and created places like New Vṛndāvana, New Rādhā-kuṇḍa, and New Māyāpur. But to do that you had to “get involved with the sense objects,” an involvement that sometimes forced you to tolerate tremendous trouble and austerities. Although you never complained about anything, once you revealed to some of your disciples: “You have no idea how much pain I went through for your sake.” In the Eighth Canto of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* it is said that this willingness to take upon oneself pain and trouble to benefit others is the highest form of worship of the Supreme Lord. This was the example of Lord *Nityānanda* Prabhu, the original guru, who at great risk saved Jagāi and Mādhāi. And this was the example of Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī Ṭhākura. And this was your example, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

How could you undertake such a task and tolerate all that pain from the adverse conditions you had to encounter in your preaching? You yourself would humbly answer that your only qualification was that you had faith in the holy name and tried to be faithful to the order of your spiritual master—a statement that is true but that concealed a greater secret.

Once a reporter asked you, “Do you believe that Kṛṣṇa exists?”

You answered, “ Believe? I *know* He exists.”

Not only did you know that Kṛṣṇa exists, but you also knew how wonderful Kṛṣṇa is, how merciful Lord Caitanya is to the lost people of Kali-yuga, and, last but not the least, how wonderful the nectar of pure devotion is. Deeply realizing the value of the treasure of love of Godhead, you gave your life in old age and endured all kinds of opposition so you could share this treasure with everybody. In this way you showed the way of a *sādhu* in Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement. And not only that, but you even empowered us to follow in your footsteps and also become *Gaurāṅga* *sādhus*. And because of your example and your instructions, here we are staying in the capital of the most secular country in the world, filled with all kinds of attractive and repellant sense objects, assisting you, the supreme *Gaurāṅga* *sādhu,* in preaching. There could not be a greater fortune that this. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you.

Your servants at the Hare Krishna Centre in Stockholm, Sweden.

## Togo

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to you! And all glories to your glorious mission!

It is a great honor for us to have this opportunity to say something in praise of you on this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, all other genuine gurus take shelter at your lotus feet. The teachings of other so-called gurus, manufactured by themselves, give us “knowledge” that keeps us in this material world. They try to lure innocent spiritual seekers and capture them in the trap of the flowery language of the *Vedas* and then keep them prisoners under the illusory energy, *māyā*.

But you came from the spiritual realm with real transcendental knowledge to save us by opening our spiritual eyes. There is nothing better to know than that we are not the body and that our duty as a spirit soul is to serve the Supreme Soul. The part must serve the whole. It is common sense, and it looks so simple, but unfortunately common sense is not common. Many people cannot see and understand this simple fact.

Now, by your mercy, the root cause of our suffering has been removed by your teachings. Ignorance of who we are is the root cause of our miseries in this world.

You are known as Abhaya Caraṇāravinda Bhaktivedanta Swami *Prabhupāda*, *Abhaya* means fearless—so you fearlessly spread the message Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu to every part of the world, without compromise. You spoke the truth to all the materialistic so-called big leaders in society. One of the six purposes of ISKCON is to expose the faults of materialism. Others could not do it because they are part of this material world, but you did it because you are a Vaikuṇṭha man who came to save us.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, O Grandfather, we pray to learn how to cry at your lotus feet, service to which is the only way out of our illusory adventure.

We are very proud of being your grandsons, passengers on your transcendental boat, in which you are taking us to the other side of the ocean of miseries. We will ever be grateful to you.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please cast your merciful glance upon these poor servants of yours, who are misled by our unsteady mind, so that one day we will know how to serve you better.

The servants of your servants in Togo.  
*(*written by Cārudeṣṇa das)

## Trivandrum

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

We are the flowers of the garland of Śrīla Prabhupāda.  
Springing in the cracks of the asphalt,  
in the clefts of the walls,
